[P21 1] Definition and Acronyms
[TABLE block 22]
Acronym / Term | Definition
BeefCare (BCMS) | On-farm Beef Cattle Care Management System (SEP490-G35).
BF / FE / UC / SCR | Business Flow / Feature / Use Case / Screen.
BR | Business Rule (GBR general, BR-D data).
Tenant | One farm workspace with its own data.
Farm Owner, Farm Manager, Farm Technician | Farm roles. The Owner owns the farm and has every Manager permission; the Manager runs daily records; the Technician views.
CS, PM, SA | Customer Support, Platform Manager, System Admin: platform staff roles.
QR tag | Cattle ear tag whose QR opens the cattle profile for farm members.
Production purpose | Why the farm raises the cattle (e.g. breeding, fattening); set by the Owner.
Life stage | UNWEANED_CALF, WEANED_CALF, YOUNG_STOCK or ADULT.
Herd entry voucher | Receipt of cattle into the farm by purchase or birth (PN-YYYY-nnnn), with lines, total value and amounts paid.
Herd exit voucher | Departure of cattle by sale, cull, death or other (PX-YYYY-nnnn), with lines, receivable and collections.
BCS | Body Condition Score (1.0–5.0).
Ration | Planned kg of each feed per head and session for its pens.
Withdrawal period | Days after a treatment or vaccination when meat must not be sold.
Payment request | A 24-hour transfer instruction for one farm and term, with payment reference and VietQR.
[/TABLE]
[P24 1] I. Overall Requirements
[P25 2] 1. Context Diagram
[P26 ] BeefCare is a web application for beef cattle farms of about 150–300 head. It keeps one record per animal from herd entry to herd exit; staff identify an animal by scanning its QR ear tag.
[P27 ] Each farm is a separate tenant and pays its own subscription. Platform staff run the service and never see cattle records.
[P29 ] Figure: Context Diagram of BeefCare
[P31 ] Table 1-1 External entities and information exchanged
[TABLE block 32]
External entity | Direction | Main information exchanged
Guest | In / Out | Views the landing page; registers and signs in.
Farm Owner | In / Out | Manages the farm, members, subscription, herd entry and exit and records care; receives dashboard, reports and reminders.
Farm Manager | In / Out | Records herd entry, care, feeding and catalogues; receives dashboard, reports and reminders.
Farm Technician | Out | Views cattle, care, rations, reminders and announcements.
Customer Support | In / Out | Handles support tickets.
Platform Manager | In / Out | Manages farm service, payments, prices and platform cash records.
System Admin | In / Out | Manages accounts, staff access and audit history.
Google Identity Provider | In / Out | Google sign-in.
SePay | In / Out | Bank transactions for subscription payments; VietQR.
Email Delivery Service | Out | Invitation, verification, password and notification emails.
[/TABLE]
[P34 2] 2. Main Business Flow
[P35 3] 2.1 Farm Onboarding, Subscription and Membership (BF-01)
[P36 ] Trigger: A Guest registers as a Farm Owner, or an existing Owner registers an additional farm or renews the subscription of an owned farm.
[P37 ] End condition: The farm is ACTIVE with an accepted purchased period, and each invited staff member has an ACTIVE membership (or the invitation is cancelled or expired).
[P39 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-01 – Farm Onboarding, Subscription and Membership
[TABLE block 41]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Register Account and Farm | The Guest signs up with Google or with a verified email and password, completes the Owner profile and registers a farm; an existing Owner registers another farm or opens an owned farm to renew. | Farm Owner
2 | Save Farm and Owner Membership | The system creates the farm in PENDING_PAYMENT with its single Owner membership and opens plan selection. | System
3 | Select Term and Review | The Owner selects 1, 2 or 3 years and reviews farm, term, amount and price version, or reopens a valid request. | Farm Owner
4 | Create or Reuse Payment Request | The system issues one immutable 24-hour request with amount, receiving account, unique payment reference and VietQR, or returns the unexpired one. | System
5 | Transfer the Amount | The Owner transfers the exact amount with the payment reference from a banking app. | Farm Owner
6 | Send Bank Evidence | SePay sends the transaction to the BeefCare webhook. | SePay
7 | Payment Eligible? | The system authenticates the evidence and checks direction, amount, receiving account, payment reference, bank-paid time within the request window and that the evidence is not yet allocated. Yes → Step 9; No → Step 8. | System
8 | Keep for Review | The system keeps the evidence for PM review; the request stays PENDING or becomes EXPIRED at its deadline. | System
9 | Confirm PAID and Activate | The system records PAID once, creates the purchased period, sets the farm ACTIVE (unless suspended), publishes the receipt and notifies the Owner, who then enters the farm Overview. | System
10 | Invite Member | The Owner enters the invitee's email and chooses Farm Manager or Farm Technician; the role's fixed permissions are shown. | Farm Owner
11 | Create Invitation and Send Email | The system checks service, email and existing membership, creates a PENDING invitation with the validity chosen by the Owner (default 7 days) and sends the email. | System
12 | Open Link and Sign In | The invitee opens the link, signs in with Google or a verified email and password, reviews the farm and role and selects Accept. | Invitee
13 | Invitation Valid? | The system checks that the invitation is PENDING and unexpired, the farm is ACTIVE and the signed-in email equals the invited email. Yes → Step 14; No → end. | System
14 | Create Membership | The system creates exactly one membership with the role, marks the invitation ACCEPTED, notifies the Owner and opens the farm. | System
[/TABLE]
[P43 3] 2.2 Farm Setup and Reference Configuration (BF-02)
[P44 ] Trigger: A farm becomes ACTIVE for the first time, or the Owner or a Manager needs to change pens, catalogues or parameters.
[P45 ] End condition: The farm information, pens, catalogues and parameters are saved as valid versions and are available for herd entry, care recording and reminders.
[P47 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-02 – Farm Setup and Reference Configuration
[TABLE block 49]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Update Farm Information | The Owner completes registration, contact and operating information (shift start, operating notes). | Farm Owner
2 | Configure Pens | The Owner or a Manager creates or updates pens with code, name, capacity and advisory reception criteria, and can stop new placements in a pen. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
3 | Maintain Catalogues | The Owner or a Manager maintains breeds, vaccines, reference medicines and production purposes. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
4 | Configure Parameters | The Owner or a Manager sets life-stage ages, breeding conditions, gestation length, pregnancy-check interval, breeding window, estrus cycle, postpartum interval, BCS scale, weighing interval and reminder lead times, with a source note. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
5 | Validate | The system checks the fields, uniqueness, the farm context and the user's role. | System
6 | Valid? | The system decides whether the change is valid. Yes → Step 7; No → Step 5. | System
7 | Save with Version | The system saves the change (a new version for criteria and parameters), recomputes future reminders and keeps all recorded events unchanged. | System
[/TABLE]
[P51 3] 2.3 Cattle Lifecycle: Herd Entry, Placement and Herd Exit (BF-03)
[P52 ] Trigger: Cattle are bought or brought into the farm, live calves are waiting for registration, or the Owner sells, culls or loses animals.
[P53 ] End condition: Each received animal has one permanent identity, profile, pen placement and history from a posted herd entry voucher; each departed animal is EXITED with a read-only record on a confirmed herd exit voucher; the amounts payable and receivable are recorded (or the voucher is voided).
[P55 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-03 – Cattle Lifecycle: Herd Entry, Placement and Herd Exit
[TABLE block 57]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Open Herd Entry Voucher | The Owner or a Manager selects "Create entry voucher" on Herd Entry Vouchers, or opens the pending calves from the list or from its reminder. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
2 | Entry Type? | The user chooses a purchase voucher or a farm-born calf voucher. Purchase → Step 3; Farm-born → Step 4. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
3 | Enter Purchase Voucher | The user enters the entry date, seller, quarantine certificate, vehicle and note; adds one line per cattle by hand or from Excel (ear tag or "tag later", breed, sex, estimated birth month, life stage, purpose, entry weight, receiving pen, price, declarations at entry); chooses per-head (default) or per-kg pricing; enters the transport fee, amount paid, method and due date. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
4 | Select Pending Calves | The user selects draft calves; dam, sire, breed, sex and birth weight come from the calving and are read-only; the user sets the receiving pen and purpose of each calf. No price or payment. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
5 | Check Voucher | The system checks tags, receiving pens (INACTIVE or full blocks; unmet criteria warn), dates, that each calf is still pending, and warns when a purchase has no quarantine certificate. Errors → Step 3 or 4. | System
6 | Post Voucher and Create Cattle | For each line, in one transaction, the system creates the Internal Cattle ID, display code, profile, herd entry, history declarations, first pen assignment and QR tag; the voucher becomes POSTED with its totals and initial payment; the calf reminder is resolved and matching vaccination schedules are listed. | System
7 | Record Payment | The user records later payments until nothing is payable. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
8 | Print or Replace QR Tag | The user prints the QR tags of the voucher, now or later; a lost or damaged tag is replaced without changing the identity. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
9 | Update Stage or Purpose | During the animal's life the user confirms each life-stage change (Unweaned calf → Weaned calf → Young stock → Adult) with effective date and reason; only the Owner changes the production purpose, whose purpose type decides the animal's herd group. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
10 | Move to Another Pen | The user selects one animal (from its profile) or several animals (from the pen detail), the destination pen, move date and reason. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
11 | Move Allowed? | The system checks the pen and each animal: INACTIVE pen or not enough free places → blocked; criteria not met → warning, continue only with a reason. Yes → Step 12; No → Step 9 or 13. | System
12 | Save Pen Move | The system closes the current assignment, opens the new one and updates occupancy and pen history. | System
13 | Open Herd Exit Voucher | The Owner creates an exit voucher (Sale, Cull or Other) and adds ACTIVE cattle from a filtered list or by QR scan. The Owner may also record a death or loss directly, or open the PENDING voucher created from a health case. | Farm Owner
14 | Enter Lines and Amounts | The Owner enters the exit date, buyer or destination, transport certificate, vehicle and note; the exit weight of each line (default the latest weight, marked UT when estimated) and the unit price; deductions, deposit collected and due date. For a death: cause and carcass disposal, no amounts. | Farm Owner
15 | Review Checks and Confirm | The Owner reviews the checks per cattle (meat withdrawal period, OPEN and CHRONIC health cases, future doses and reminders, open pregnancy, draft calves of the dam), ticks the confirmation and confirms, or dismisses a pending voucher with a reason. | Farm Owner
16 | Record Exit | For each line the system sets EXITED, closes the pen assignment, cancels future doses and reminders, closes OPEN and CHRONIC health cases and any open pregnancy and locks the record; the voucher becomes CONFIRMED. | System
[/TABLE]
[P59 3] 2.4 Daily Care: Growth, Vaccination and Health (BF-04)
[P60 ] Trigger: The Farm Owner or a Farm Manager opens the day's reminders (weighing, vaccination, follow-ups) or an abnormal sign is observed in the pen.
[P61 ] End condition: The care performed is recorded against each animal, reminders are updated, and any death or loss is pending the Owner's herd-exit decision.
[P63 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-04 – Daily Care: Growth, Vaccination and Health
[TABLE block 65]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Review Reminders | The Owner or a Manager (and Technicians, read-only) open the home screen or Farm Reminders: doses due or overdue, weighing due, open cases. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager / Farm Technician
2 | Identify the Animal | The user scans the QR tag or searches, and opens the profile. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager / Farm Technician
3 | Care Activity? | The Owner or a Manager chooses the activity to record. Growth → Step 4; Health → Step 6; Vaccination → Step 10. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
4 | Record Weight and/or BCS | The Owner or a Manager records time, weight, method, basis and/or BCS; the measurer may be a Technician. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
5 | Update Growth Indicators | The system saves the assessment, recalculates current weight and ADG, resolves the weighing reminder and may suggest a pen move or a life-stage change. | System
6 | Record Abnormal Signs | The Owner or a Manager records observed time, temperature, signs, severity and observer, and opens a new case or adds to an OPEN or CHRONIC case; the health state becomes at least Monitoring. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
7 | Record Treatment and Follow-up | The Owner or a Manager records each treatment actually given (medicine, dose, route, time, withdrawal days) and follow-up observations; the health state becomes Under treatment and the meat withdrawal date is updated. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
8 | Case Outcome? | The Owner or a Manager keeps the case OPEN, moves it to CHRONIC (long-term monitoring) or closes it as RECOVERED, DIED or MISSING. OPEN or CHRONIC → Step 7; DIED/MISSING → Step 9; RECOVERED → Step 14. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
9 | Create Pending Exit | The system creates a PENDING herd exit for the Owner; the animal stays ACTIVE until the Owner decides. | System
10 | Manage Vaccination Schedule | The Owner or a Manager creates or updates a schedule: vaccine, doses, interval, booster and target cattle by pen, herd group, life stage or selection. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
11 | Evaluate Doses | The system derives each dose as upcoming, due or overdue and raises the vaccination reminder. | System
12 | Can Vaccinate Now? | For each selected animal the user decides whether the dose is given now. Yes → Step 13; No → Step 14. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
13 | Record Vaccination | The Owner or a Manager records vaccine, time, dose, route, withdrawal days and vaccinator for one animal or a selected group; the system saves one record per animal, closes the doses and updates the meat withdrawal date. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
14 | Update Timeline and Reports | The system adds the records to each animal's timeline, the dashboard and reports. | System
[/TABLE]
[P67 3] 2.5 Reproduction: Estrus to Calf Registration (BF-05)
[P68 ] Trigger: A female shows estrus, a reproduction reminder falls due (breeding window, pregnancy check, upcoming calving, postpartum), or a calving occurs.
[P69 ] End condition: The calving and every calf outcome are recorded and each live calf is registered (directly or after draft confirmation), or the pregnancy is closed as lost.
[P71 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-05 – Reproduction: Estrus to Calf Registration
[TABLE block 73]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Open Female Profile | The Owner or a Manager opens the female from Reproduction Worklist, Cattle List, a reminder or a QR scan and views the Reproduction tab: reproductive status, the actions it allows and the breeding eligibility indicator. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
2 | Record Estrus | The Owner or a Manager records the observed time, observer and description. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
3 | Suggest Breeding Window | The system shows the breeding window (estrus + 8 to 12 hours by default) and cancels any tentative expected calving date of an earlier breeding. | System
4 | Record Breeding | The Owner or a Manager records bred time, method, sire or semen, performer and the optional linked estrus. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
5 | Set Tentative Date | The system sets the TENTATIVE expected calving date and the pregnancy-check reminder (breeding + 28 days by default). | System
6 | Record Pregnancy Check | The Owner or a Manager records time, result, method and examiner (often an external veterinarian). | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
7 | Result? | The system evaluates the result. PREGNANT → Step 8; NOT_PREGNANT → Step 1; INCONCLUSIVE → Step 6. | System
8 | Open Pregnancy Episode | The system opens the episode, marks the expected date CONFIRMED and prepares the calving reminder (30 days before by default). | System
9 | Record Pregnancy Update | The Owner or a Manager records an observation, a complication (optionally linked to a health case) or a loss. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
10 | Pregnancy Lost? | The system checks the update type. Yes → Step 1; No → Step 11. | System
11 | Record Calving and Calf Outcomes | The Owner or a Manager records birth time, attendant, course, dam condition and one outcome per calf; for each live calf enters sex, weight, tag/QR, pen and chooses Save as draft calf or Register now. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
12 | Save Calving | The system closes the episode as CALVED, sets the dam POST_CALVING, resolves the calving reminder and prepares the postpartum reminder. | System
13 | Register Now? | The system applies the choice made for each live calf. Register now → Step 16; Draft → Step 14; no live calf → end. | System
14 | Save Draft Calf | The system saves the calf as PENDING_CONFIRMATION and creates a reminder for the Owner and Managers. | System
15 | Confirm Draft Calf | The Owner or a Manager reviews and confirms the draft calf. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
16 | Create Calf Record | The system creates the calf's Internal Cattle ID, links dam and sire, assigns the pen. | System
[/TABLE]
[P75 3] 2.6 Farm Coordination and Monitoring (BF-06)
[P76 ] Trigger: The Owner or a Manager needs to inform staff, or wants to review the herd situation; or a member opens an animal in the field.
[P77 ] End condition: Recipients have received the announcement version; the Owner or Manager has reviewed the indicators and opened the records or actions needed.
[P79 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-06 – Farm Coordination and Monitoring
[TABLE block 81]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Compose Announcement | The author writes title, body and recipients: the Owner addresses Managers and Technicians; a Manager addresses Technicians. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
2 | Save Draft | The system validates the audience and saves a draft visible only to its author. | System
3 | Review and Publish | The author reviews content and resolved recipients and publishes once. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
4 | Deliver and Notify | The system saves the version and recipient snapshot and notifies the recipients. | System
5 | Read Announcements and Reminders | Recipients read announcements and reminders and open each reminder's source record. | Farm Manager / Farm Technician
6 | Correction Needed? | The author decides whether the published content needs a correction. Yes → Step 4; No → end. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
7 | Open Dashboard or Report | The Owner or a Manager selects period and filters, or a report type and criteria. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
8 | Aggregate Indicators | The system computes herd, growth, health, vaccination and reproduction indicators and attention lists (including draft calves and pending death vouchers) with generation time. | System
9 | Drill Down or Scan | The user drills down from an indicator, searches, or scans an ear tag. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager / Farm Technician
10 | Show Profile and Timeline | The system shows the profile and timeline in date order, with occurrence and entry time and details limited by role. | System
11 | Action Needed? | The user decides whether there is something to record or decide. Yes → Step 12; No → end. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
12 | Open Permitted Use Case | The Manager opens a recording use case; the Owner opens an Owner action (herd exit, purpose change). | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
[/TABLE]
[P83 3] 2.7 Customer Support and Internal Requests (BF-07)
[P84 ] Trigger: A Farm Owner submits a support ticket about their account or an owned farm.
[P85 ] End condition: The ticket is CLOSED after the Owner explicitly confirmed the latest resolution and no internal request is open.
[P87 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-07 – Customer Support and Internal Requests
[TABLE block 89]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Submit Ticket | The Owner submits a ticket with category, scope (account or owned farm), subject and description; the system saves it OPEN with priority NORMAL. | Farm Owner
2 | Claim Ticket | A CS claims the ticket from the queue (or receives a handover) and becomes responsible; status IN_PROGRESS. | Customer Support
3 | Investigate and Reply | The assigned CS sets category and priority, replies PUBLIC and writes CS-only notes. | Customer Support
4 | Next Action? | The CS chooses the next action. Ask Owner → Step 5; internal help → Step 6; ready → Step 8. | Customer Support
5 | Owner Replies | The CS sets WAITING_CUSTOMER; the Owner replies; the system returns the ticket to IN_PROGRESS. | Farm Owner
6 | Send Internal Request | The CS sends an OPEN request to a named PM or SA with the question and permitted context. | Customer Support
7 | Handle Internal Request | The named PM or SA performs the authorized work in their own use cases and returns a sanitized result (DONE). | Platform Manager / System Admin
8 | Record Resolution | The CS records a PUBLIC resolution (new version) when no internal request is OPEN; the system sets RESOLVED and notifies the Owner. | Customer Support
9 | Issue Resolved? | The Owner confirms the latest resolution, or reopens with a reason. Confirm → Step 10; Reopen → Step 3. | Farm Owner
10 | Close Ticket | The CS reviews the Owner's confirmation and closes; the system rechecks and commits CLOSED. | Customer Support
[/TABLE]
[P91 3] 2.8 Platform Administration: Payments, Finance and Access (BF-08)
[P92 ] Trigger: SePay delivers a bank transaction that cannot be confirmed automatically, or a Platform Manager or System Admin starts an administrative task.
[P93 ] End condition: Each evidence is accepted, held or rejected with a reason; farm service, prices and finance records are updated with history; account and staff access changes are applied and audited.
[P95 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-08 – Platform Administration: Payments, Finance and Access
[TABLE block 97]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Which Task? | The flow starts from a bank transaction, a PM task or an SA task. Bank evidence → Step 2; PM → Step 4; SA → Step 11. | System
2 | Receive Bank Evidence | SePay delivers the transaction; the system authenticates and stores it once. | SePay
3 | Evaluate Eligibility | The system applies the shared acceptance rules. Eligible → Step 7; otherwise → Step 5. | System
4 | Choose PM Operation | The PM chooses farm service, payment reconciliation, plan prices or finance. Service/prices → Step 6; reconciliation → Step 5; finance → Step 8. | Platform Manager
5 | Review Evidence | The PM reviews the evidence and candidate request and records ACCEPT, HOLD or REJECT with a reason; ACCEPT re-runs the shared rules. | Platform Manager
6 | Manage Service and Prices | The PM suspends or restores a farm with a reason, or publishes 1/2/3-year prices now or at a scheduled time. | Platform Manager
7 | Confirm PAID and Publish Receipt | The system records PAID once, creates the purchased period, publishes the receipt and notifies the Owner. | System
8 | Manage Manual Finance Records | The PM creates, edits, posts, discards or voids manual income and expense records. | Platform Manager
9 | Run Cash Report | The PM selects period and farm; the system builds a snapshot of receipts plus posted income minus expense. | Platform Manager
10 | Export Report | The PM exports the displayed snapshot to XLSX. | Platform Manager
11 | Manage Accounts and Staff Access | The SA suspends or restores accounts and grants, changes, revokes or restores CS, PM or SA access with a reason. | System Admin
12 | Review Audit History | The SA searches the allowlisted platform audit history. | System Admin
[/TABLE]
[P99 3] 2.9 Feed Supply and Daily Feeding (BF-09)
[P100 ] Trigger: Feed is delivered by a supplier, a feeding session is due, a ration has to change, the stock of a feed reaches its minimum level or a stock count is planned.
[P101 ] End condition: Feed stock reflects every posted receipt, adjustment and feeding record; each pen's feeding sessions are recorded against its effective ration; consumption and cost are available for the period.
[P103 ] Figure: Workflow of BF-09 – Feed Supply and Daily Feeding
[TABLE block 105]
Step | Step Name | Detailed Description | Role
1 | Review Feed Stock | The Owner or a Manager opens Feed Management; Feed Stock shows the stock, minimum level, average daily use, days of cover and lots near expiry of each feed. Receipt → Step 2; ration → Step 5; feeding → Step 7; stock count → Step 11. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
2 | Enter Feed Receipt | The user enters the supplier, receipt date, invoice or delivery note, receiver and storage place, and one line per feed (lot, expiry date, quantity in the purchase unit, conversion to kg, unit price), the transport fee, amount paid and due date. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
3 | Check Receipt | The system checks that each feed is ACTIVE, quantities are positive, prices not negative, expiry dates after the receipt date and the date not in the future. Invalid → Step 2. | System
4 | Post Receipt | The system adds the stock in kg, recalculates the weighted average cost, records the supplier balance and clears the low-stock reminder of feeds above their minimum level. | System
5 | Create or Update Ration | The user defines the ration name, herd group, pens, sessions per day and kg per head per session of each feed, the effective date (not earlier than tomorrow) and the basis. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
6 | Save Ration Version | The system checks that each pen has at most one effective ration per day, saves the version, ends the previous version the day before and shows the estimated daily quantity and cost. | System
7 | Open Feeding Session | The Owner or a Manager selects the pen and session; the system shows the effective ration, the planned quantity of each feed (ration × current headcount) and its stock. A pen without an effective ration can still be recorded with actual feeds. Technicians follow Today's Rations on the phone. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager / Farm Technician
8 | Enter Actual Quantities | The Owner or a Manager enters the actual kg of each feed (or fills them from the plan), extra feeds outside the ration, leftovers, the member who fed (may be a Technician) and a note. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
9 | Valid? | The system checks that no record exists for the pen, date and session, the date is not in the future and no feed exceeds its stock. Yes → Step 10; No → Step 8. | System
10 | Save and Deduct Stock | The system saves the feeding record, deducts each feed from stock at its average cost, resolves the feeding reminder and raises the low-stock reminder when a feed reaches its minimum level. A correction or void with a reason adjusts stock by the difference. | System
11 | Count Stock | The user enters the counted stock of each feed and a reason for every difference. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
12 | Post Adjustment | The system records one stock movement per difference, valued at the average cost; no stock becomes negative and earlier documents are not changed. | System
13 | Review Consumption and Cost | The user selects a period and views consumption, cost per head per day and the variance from the rations by herd group, pen or feed. | Farm Owner / Farm Manager
[/TABLE]
[P107 2] 3. Conceptual Data Model
[P108 3] 3.1 Entity Relationship Diagram
[P110 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – Overview
[P112 ] Table 3.1-1 Subject areas and conceptual entities
[TABLE block 113]
# | Subject area | Conceptual entities | Detail kept with the entity
A | Users and Farm Membership | User Account, Farm Owner, Farm Manager, Farm Technician, Customer Support, Platform Manager, System Admin, Farm, Farm Invitation | User Account: Sign-in Method; Farm: Farm Membership, Farm Service Change, Farm Parameter Version
B | Farm Reference Data | Breed, Production Purpose, Vaccine, Medicine, Pen | Pen: Pen Reception Criteria
C | Cattle and Herd Movement | Cattle, Cattle QR Tag, Herd Entry Voucher, Herd Exit Voucher, Trading Partner, Voucher Payment | Cattle: Cattle Profile Change, Pen Assignment; Herd Entry Voucher: Herd Entry, Pre-entry History Declaration; Herd Exit Voucher: Herd Exit
D | Care and Health | Growth Assessment, Vaccination Schedule, Vaccination Record, Health Case, Treatment | Vaccination Schedule: Scheduled Dose; Health Case: Health Observation
E | Reproduction | Estrus Event, Breeding Event, Pregnancy Check, Pregnancy Episode, Calving Event, Calf Outcome | Pregnancy Episode: Pregnancy Update
F | Feed Management | Feed Item, Feed Receipt, Stock Adjustment, Ration, Feeding Record | Feed Receipt: Feed Receipt Line; Stock Adjustment: Stock Adjustment Line; Ration: Ration Component; Feeding Record: Feeding Record Line
G | Farm Announcements | Farm Announcement | Farm Announcement: Announcement Version, Announcement Delivery
H | Subscription and Platform Finance | Plan Price Version, Subscription, Payment Request, Bank Transfer Evidence, Manual Finance Record | Payment Request: Purchased Period; Bank Transfer Evidence: Evidence Review
I | Customer Support | Support Ticket, Ticket Message, Ticket Resolution, Internal Request | Support Ticket: Ticket History Event; Ticket Resolution: Resolution Acknowledgment
[/TABLE]
[P116 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – A. Users and Farm Membership
[P119 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – B. Farm Reference Data
[P122 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – C. Cattle and Herd Movement
[P125 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – D. Care and Health
[P128 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – E. Reproduction
[P131 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – F. Feed Management
[P134 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – G. Farm Announcements
[P137 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – H. Subscription and Platform Finance
[P140 ] Figure: Conceptual ERD – I. Customer Support
[P142 ] Table 3.1-2 Relationships
[P143 ] Cardinality is written as "Entity A side : Entity B side" (1, 0..1, 1..*, 0..*).
[TABLE block 144]
# | Entity A | Relationship | Entity B | Cardinality | Business meaning
R01 | Farm Owner / Farm Manager / Farm Technician / Customer Support / Platform Manager / System Admin | is a | User Account | 0..1 : 1 | A role belongs to one account; one account may hold several roles.
R02 | System Admin | suspends or restores | User Account | 0..* : 0..* | Suspension or restoration with a reason.
R03 | Farm Owner | owns | Farm | 1 : 1..* | An Owner may own several farms; each farm has one Owner.
R04 | Farm Manager | works in | Farm | 0..* : 1..* | MANAGER membership; one person has one role in each farm.
R05 | Farm Technician | works in | Farm | 0..* : 1..* | TECHNICIAN membership; read-only access to farm records.
R06 | Farm | issues | Farm Invitation | 1 : 0..* | Invitations to join the farm with a role.
R07 | User Account | accepts | Farm Invitation | 0..1 : 0..* | Accepting creates the membership.
R08 | Platform Manager | suspends or restores | Farm | 0..* : 0..* | Suspension or restoration with a reason.
R09 | System Admin | grants access to | Customer Support | 1..* : 0..* | Grants or revokes CS access.
R10 | System Admin | grants access to | Platform Manager | 1..* : 0..* | Grants or revokes PM and SA access.
R11 | Farm | maintains | Breed | 1 : 0..* | Farm catalogue.
R12 | Farm | maintains | Production Purpose | 1 : 0..* | Farm catalogue with a fixed purpose type.
R13 | Farm | maintains | Vaccine | 1 : 0..* | Farm catalogue.
R14 | Farm | maintains | Medicine | 1 : 0..* | Farm catalogue.
R15 | Farm | contains | Pen | 1 : 0..* | Pens and care areas of the farm.
R16 | Farm | raises | Cattle | 1 : 0..* | Every animal belongs to one farm.
R17 | Breed | classifies | Cattle | 1 : 0..* | Breed of the animal.
R18 | Production Purpose | is assigned to | Cattle | 1 : 0..* | Why the farm raises the animal.
R19 | Pen | houses | Cattle | 0..1 : 0..* | Current pen of the cattle.
R20 | Cattle (dam) | is the mother of | Cattle | 0..1 : 0..* | Known dam in the farm.
R21 | Cattle (sire) | is the father of | Cattle | 0..1 : 0..* | Known sire in the farm.
R22 | Cattle | carries | Cattle QR Tag | 1 : 1..* | One ACTIVE tag at a time; replaced tags are kept.
R23 | Herd Entry Voucher | brings in | Cattle | 1 : 1..* | Each animal enters the herd through exactly one voucher.
R24 | Herd Exit Voucher | removes | Cattle | 0..1 : 1..* | An animal leaves through at most one effective exit voucher.
R25 | Trading Partner | sells in | Herd Entry Voucher | 0..1 : 0..* | Seller of a purchase; none for farm-born calves.
R26 | Trading Partner | buys in | Herd Exit Voucher | 0..1 : 0..* | Buyer of a sale or cull.
R27 | Herd Entry Voucher / Herd Exit Voucher / Feed Receipt | is settled by | Voucher Payment | 1 : 0..* | Payments and collections recorded on the voucher.
R28 | Calf Outcome | is registered as | Cattle | 0..1 : 0..1 | A live calf becomes at most one cattle.
R29 | Cattle | is assessed in | Growth Assessment | 1 : 0..* | Weight and body condition history.
R30 | Vaccine | is planned in | Vaccination Schedule | 1 : 0..* | One vaccine per schedule.
R31 | Vaccination Schedule | covers | Cattle | 0..* : 0..* | Planned doses for the targeted animals.
R32 | Cattle | receives | Vaccination Record | 1 : 0..* | Vaccinations given on the farm or declared at entry.
R33 | Vaccine | is given in | Vaccination Record | 0..1 : 0..* | Vaccine used; may be empty for a historical record.
R34 | Vaccination Schedule | is fulfilled by | Vaccination Record | 0..1 : 0..* | A planned dose is fulfilled by an on-farm vaccination.
R35 | Cattle | has | Health Case | 1 : 0..* | Health problems of the animal.
R36 | Health Case | is treated by | Treatment | 1 : 0..* | Treatments actually given.
R37 | Medicine | is used in | Treatment | 1 : 0..* | Medicine from the farm catalogue.
R38 | Vaccination Record | causes | Health Case | 0..1 : 0..* | Post-vaccination reaction.
R39 | Health Case | proposes | Herd Exit Voucher | 0..1 : 0..1 | A case closed as died or missing proposes a death voucher.
R40 | Cattle (female) | shows | Estrus Event | 1 : 0..* | Observed heats.
R41 | Cattle (female) | is bred in | Breeding Event | 1 : 0..* | Natural service or AI.
R42 | Cattle (sire) | serves in | Breeding Event | 0..1 : 0..* | Farm bull used for natural service.
R43 | Estrus Event | leads to | Breeding Event | 0..1 : 0..* | Breeding after an observed heat.
R44 | Breeding Event | is checked by | Pregnancy Check | 0..1 : 0..* | Check of the latest breeding.
R45 | Pregnancy Check | confirms | Pregnancy Episode | 1 : 0..1 | A PREGNANT result opens the episode.
R46 | Cattle (female) | carries | Pregnancy Episode | 1 : 0..* | At most one open pregnancy at a time.
R47 | Pregnancy Episode | ends with | Calving Event | 0..1 : 0..1 | Calving closes the pregnancy.
R48 | Cattle (dam) | gives birth in | Calving Event | 1 : 0..* | Calvings of the dam.
R49 | Calving Event | produces | Calf Outcome | 1 : 1..* | One outcome per calf.
R50 | Farm | stocks | Feed Item | 1 : 0..* | Feed catalogue and stock.
R51 | Trading Partner | supplies | Feed Receipt | 1 : 0..* | Feed supplier.
R52 | Feed Receipt | includes | Feed Item | 0..* : 1..* | Received quantity, lot and price per feed.
R53 | Stock Adjustment | counts | Feed Item | 0..* : 1..* | Counted stock and difference per feed.
R54 | Ration | specifies | Feed Item | 0..* : 1..* | Planned kg per head and session.
R55 | Ration | applies to | Pen | 0..* : 1..* | One effective ration per pen and day.
R56 | Pen | is fed in | Feeding Record | 1 : 0..* | One record per pen, date and session.
R57 | Ration | plans | Feeding Record | 0..1 : 0..* | Ration effective at the feeding date.
R58 | Feeding Record | consumes | Feed Item | 0..* : 1..* | Actual kg deducted from stock.
R59 | Farm | publishes | Farm Announcement | 1 : 0..* | Announcements to farm members.
R60 | Farm Owner | authors | Farm Announcement | 0..1 : 0..* | Announcement written by the Owner.
R61 | Farm Manager | authors | Farm Announcement | 0..1 : 0..* | Announcement written by a Manager.
R62 | Farm Announcement | is delivered to | Farm Manager | 0..* : 0..* | Owner announcements may address Managers.
R63 | Farm Announcement | is delivered to | Farm Technician | 0..* : 0..* | Owner and Manager announcements may address Technicians.
R64 | Farm | has | Subscription | 1 : 0..1 | One subscription per farm.
R65 | Platform Manager | maintains | Plan Price Version | 1 : 0..* | Plan prices are versioned by the PM.
R66 | Subscription | is paid by | Payment Request | 1 : 0..* | Each accepted request grants one service period.
R67 | Plan Price Version | prices | Payment Request | 1 : 0..* | Price version used by the request.
R68 | Bank Transfer Evidence | funds | Payment Request | 0..1 : 0..1 | One accepted transfer funds one request.
R69 | Platform Manager | reviews | Bank Transfer Evidence | 0..1 : 0..* | Manual reconciliation of transfers not matched automatically.
R70 | Farm | is attributed | Manual Finance Record | 0..1 : 0..* | General records have no farm.
R71 | Platform Manager | records | Manual Finance Record | 1 : 0..* | Basic platform cash records.
R72 | Farm Owner | submits | Support Ticket | 1 : 0..* | Requester of the ticket.
R73 | Farm | concerns | Support Ticket | 0..1 : 0..* | Account-level tickets have no farm.
R74 | Customer Support | is assigned | Support Ticket | 0..1 : 0..* | Responsible Customer Support.
R75 | Support Ticket | contains | Ticket Message | 1 : 0..* | Public replies and CS-only notes.
R76 | Customer Support | writes | Ticket Message | 0..1 : 0..* | Replies and notes written by a CS.
R77 | Support Ticket | has | Ticket Resolution | 1 : 0..* | Resolution versions proposed by CS.
R78 | Customer Support | records | Ticket Resolution | 1 : 0..* | Recorded by the assigned CS.
R79 | Support Ticket | raises | Internal Request | 1 : 0..* | Requests to a named PM or SA.
R80 | Customer Support | creates | Internal Request | 1 : 0..* | Created by the assigned CS.
R81 | Platform Manager | handles | Internal Request | 0..1 : 0..* | Named PM recipient.
R82 | System Admin | handles | Internal Request | 0..1 : 0..* | Named SA recipient; each request has exactly one recipient.
[/TABLE]
[P146 3] 3.2 Entity Descriptions
[P147 4] A. Users and Farm Membership
[P148 ] Entity: User Account
[TABLE block 149]
Field | Value
Purpose | The one global account of a person, reused for every farm the person owns or joins and for any platform staff role.
Key business attributes | Full name, verified email, optional contact phone, account status, Owner registration completed (yes/no, time).
Business identity | Verified email is unique across all accounts (case-insensitive).
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ SUSPENDED (System Admin, with reason).
Detail – Sign-in Method | A way the account signs in: a Google identity or a BeefCare password.
[/TABLE]
[P151 ] Entity: Farm Owner
[TABLE block 152]
Field | Value
Purpose | The person who registers, owns and pays for a farm.
Key business attributes | User account, owned farms.
Business identity | The account that holds the OWNER membership of a farm.
Status / lifecycle | Follows the user account (ACTIVE ⇄ SUSPENDED by the System Admin).
[/TABLE]
[P154 ] Entity: Farm Manager
[TABLE block 155]
Field | Value
Purpose | A farm member who records daily operations and manages the farm's data.
Key business attributes | User account, farm, MANAGER membership.
Status / lifecycle | Membership ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE; role changed by the Owner.
[/TABLE]
[P157 ] Entity: Farm Technician
[TABLE block 158]
Field | Value
Purpose | A farm member with read-only access to farm records.
Key business attributes | User account, farm, TECHNICIAN membership.
Status / lifecycle | Membership ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE; role changed by the Owner.
[/TABLE]
[P160 ] Entity: Customer Support
[TABLE block 161]
Field | Value
Purpose | Platform staff who handle Farm Owners' support tickets.
Key business attributes | User account, platform role CS, access status, assigned tickets.
Business identity | An account with an ACTIVE CS platform role.
Status / lifecycle | Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED (System Admin, with reason).
[/TABLE]
[P163 ] Entity: Platform Manager
[TABLE block 164]
Field | Value
Purpose | Platform staff who manages farm service, subscriptions, prices, payment reconciliation and platform cash records.
Key business attributes | User account, platform role PM, access status.
Business identity | An account with an ACTIVE PM platform role.
Status / lifecycle | Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED (System Admin, with reason).
[/TABLE]
[P166 ] Entity: System Admin
[TABLE block 167]
Field | Value
Purpose | Platform staff who manages accounts, staff access and the audit history.
Key business attributes | User account, platform role SA, access status.
Business identity | An account with an ACTIVE SA platform role.
Status / lifecycle | Access ACTIVE ⇄ REVOKED (System Admin, with reason).
[/TABLE]
[P169 ] Entity: Farm
[TABLE block 170]
Field | Value
Purpose | An independent farm workspace (tenant).
Key business attributes | Farm name, province, commune, detailed address, contact person and phone, registration certificate information, expected herd size, main breeds, time zone, operating notes, service status (derived), suspension flag.
Status / lifecycle | Service status PENDING_PAYMENT → ACTIVE ⇄ EXPIRED, and SUSPENDED whenever suspended by a PM.
Detail – Farm Membership | The link between an account and one farm, carrying the person's role in that farm.
Detail – Farm Service Change | One suspension or restoration of a farm's service by a Platform Manager.
Detail – Farm Parameter Version | One version of the farm's state and reminder parameters used by suggestions and reminders.
[/TABLE]
[P172 ] Entity: Farm Invitation
[TABLE block 173]
Field | Value
Purpose | An Owner's invitation for an email address to join a farm as Farm Manager or Farm Technician; it is accepted by the User Account whose verified email equals the invited email.
Key business attributes | Farm, recipient email, role, validity (3, 7 or 14 days), expiry time, accepted account and membership.
Business identity | At most one PENDING invitation per farm + email (case-insensitive).
Status / lifecycle | PENDING → ACCEPTED, CANCELLED or EXPIRED (all final).
[/TABLE]
[P175 4] B. Farm Reference Data
[P176 ] Entity: Breed
[TABLE block 177]
Field | Value
Purpose | A cattle breed used by the farm (for example Red Zebu, Lai Sind, Red Brahman).
Key business attributes | Farm, code, name, group, number of cattle referencing it.
Business identity | Code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE.
[/TABLE]
[P179 ] Entity: Production Purpose
[TABLE block 180]
Field | Value
Purpose | The purpose for which the farm raises a cattle (for example "Breeding cow", "Fattening for sale", "Breeding bull", "Undecided").
Key business attributes | Farm, code, name, purpose type.
Business identity | Code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE.
[/TABLE]
[P182 ] Entity: Vaccine
[TABLE block 183]
Field | Value
Purpose | A vaccine reference used in schedules and vaccination records.
Key business attributes | Farm, code, name, disease prevented, manufacturer, reference dose and route, booster interval (days), meat withdrawal period (days).
Business identity | Code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE.
[/TABLE]
[P185 ] Entity: Medicine
[TABLE block 186]
Field | Value
Purpose | A reference medicine used when recording treatments.
Key business attributes | Farm, code, name, reference dose, route, meat withdrawal period (days).
Business identity | Code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE.
[/TABLE]
[P188 ] Entity: Pen
[TABLE block 189]
Field | Value
Purpose | A pen or care area of the farm in which cattle are placed.
Key business attributes | Farm, pen code, pen name, capacity, availability status, current headcount (derived), current criteria version.
Business identity | Pen code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE (with effective date and reason).
Detail – Pen Reception Criteria | One version of the advisory criteria a pen uses to check placements and to suggest pen moves.
[/TABLE]
[P191 4] C. Cattle and Herd Movement
[P192 ] Entity: Cattle
[TABLE block 193]
Field | Value
Purpose | One animal of the farm, followed from herd entry to herd exit.
Key business attributes | Internal Cattle ID, display code, ear tag number, name, sex, breed, birth date or estimated birth month with accuracy, colour and markings, photo, production purpose, life stage, herd status, exit type and date or void reason, dam, sire (internal cattle or external sire/semen code).
Business identity | Internal Cattle ID is system-generated, immutable and never reused.
Status / lifecycle | Herd status ACTIVE → EXITED (confirmed exit) → ACTIVE (voided exit); ACTIVE → VOIDED (herd entry voided; final).
Detail – Cattle Profile Change | The history of one change of production purpose, life stage, ear tag number or descriptive data.
Detail – Pen Assignment | One period during which a cattle is placed in a pen.
[/TABLE]
[P195 ] Entity: Cattle QR Tag
[TABLE block 196]
Field | Value
Purpose | A printed QR tag that identifies a cattle in the field.
Key business attributes | Cattle, tag code, replacement reason, replaced-by tag.
Business identity | Tag code is unique; at most one ACTIVE tag per cattle.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE → REPLACED (new tag issued) or RETIRED (herd entry voided); never reactivated.
[/TABLE]
[P198 ] Entity: Herd Entry Voucher
[TABLE block 199]
Field | Value
Purpose | One receipt of cattle into the farm, by purchase or as farm-born calves, grouping one or more herd entries with their value and the amounts paid.
Key business attributes | Voucher code, entry type, entry date, seller (trading partner), quarantine certificate number (PURCHASE, optional), vehicle plate, pricing basis, cattle amount, transport fee, total payable, amount paid, outstanding amount, payment due date.
Business identity | Code PN-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm and never reused.
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → POSTED → VOIDED; a DRAFT can be discarded.
Detail – Herd Entry | How and when one cattle entered the herd; one line of a herd entry voucher.
Detail – Pre-entry History Declaration | Declared history of a bought-in cattle before entry, per category.
[/TABLE]
[P201 ] Entity: Herd Exit Voucher
[TABLE block 202]
Field | Value
Purpose | One departure of cattle from the farm – sale, cull, death or missing, or other – grouping herd exits with their value and the collections from the buyer; confirmed by the Owner.
Key business attributes | Voucher code, exit type, exit date, buyer (trading partner) or destination, transport quarantine certificate, vehicle plate, reason/notes, loss kind, cause and carcass disposal (DEATH), source health case, pricing basis, cattle amount, deductions, total receivable, amount collected, outstanding amount, collection due date, reported by, confirmed by and time, dismiss or void reason, actor and time.
Business identity | Code PX-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm and never reused.
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → CONFIRMED → VOIDED (Owner); PENDING (created from a health case closed as DIED or MISSING) → CONFIRMED or DISMISSED; a DRAFT can be discarded.
Detail – Herd Exit | The end of one cattle's stay in the herd; one line of a herd exit voucher.
[/TABLE]
[P204 ] Entity: Trading Partner
[TABLE block 205]
Field | Value
Purpose | A seller, buyer, market, slaughterhouse or feed supplier the farm deals with.
Key business attributes | Name, partner roles, contact phone, area or address.
Business identity | Name is unique within the farm (case-insensitive).
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE; never deleted once referenced by a voucher.
[/TABLE]
[P207 ] Entity: Voucher Payment
[TABLE block 208]
Field | Value
Purpose | One payment or collection recorded against a voucher.
Key business attributes | Voucher, direction (pay / collect), payment date, amount, method, reference, attachment.
Status / lifecycle | RECORDED → VOIDED (with reason; a wrong payment is voided and recorded again).
[/TABLE]
[P210 4] D. Care and Health
[P211 ] Entity: Growth Assessment
[TABLE block 212]
Field | Value
Purpose | One measurement of weight and/or body condition score of a cattle.
Key business attributes | Cattle, measured time, weight (kg), method, heart girth and body length (cm, TAPE), basis, BCS and scale version, measured by.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → EXCLUDED (deleted) or VOIDED, with an optional note; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision.
[/TABLE]
[P214 ] Entity: Vaccination Schedule
[TABLE block 215]
Field | Value
Purpose | A plan to vaccinate a defined set of cattle with one vaccine.
Key business attributes | Farm, name, vaccine, target selection (pens, herd groups, life stages, individual cattle), start date, number of doses, days between doses, booster interval (months).
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE → STOPPED.
Detail – Scheduled Dose | One planned dose of one cattle in a schedule.
[/TABLE]
[P217 ] Entity: Vaccination Record
[TABLE block 218]
Field | Value
Purpose | One vaccination actually given to a cattle, or declared from its history.
Key business attributes | Cattle, vaccine (optional for a historical record) and vaccine name, administered time, dose, route, withdrawal days and end date (on-farm only), vaccinator, source, scheduled dose.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED, with reason; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision.
[/TABLE]
[P220 ] Entity: Health Case
[TABLE block 221]
Field | Value
Purpose | One health problem of a cattle, grouping its observations and treatments.
Key business attributes | Case code, cattle, opened time, main signs, suspected diagnosis (entered by the user), suspected contagious flag, severity, chronic since, outcome, closed time, closed by, linked vaccination (reaction).
Business identity | Case code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | OPEN ⇄ CHRONIC → CLOSED with an outcome; a closed case can be reopened with a reason.
Detail – Health Observation | The opening observation or a follow-up observation of a health case.
[/TABLE]
[P223 ] Entity: Treatment
[TABLE block 224]
Field | Value
Purpose | One treatment actually given within a health case.
Key business attributes | Health case, medicine (and name snapshot), dose, route, administered time (first administration), days of use (default 1), withdrawal days, withdrawal end date (derived), performer.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED, with reason; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker and a Record Revision.
[/TABLE]
[P226 4] E. Reproduction
[P227 ] Entity: Estrus Event
[TABLE block 228]
Field | Value
Purpose | An observed estrus of a female cattle.
Key business attributes | Female, observed time, observer, signs.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED (Owner or Manager, with reason); a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker.
[/TABLE]
[P230 ] Entity: Breeding Event
[TABLE block 231]
Field | Value
Purpose | An actual breeding (natural service or artificial insemination) of a female.
Key business attributes | Female, bred time, method, sire source, internal sire, sire breed, semen straw/batch code, semen supplier, performer, linked estrus, tentative expected calving date.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker.
[/TABLE]
[P233 ] Entity: Pregnancy Check
[TABLE block 234]
Field | Value
Purpose | An actual pregnancy examination result.
Key business attributes | Female, checked time, result, method, estimated months pregnant, examiner, linked breeding.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker.
[/TABLE]
[P236 ] Entity: Pregnancy Episode
[TABLE block 237]
Field | Value
Purpose | One confirmed pregnancy of a female, from the confirming check to calving or loss.
Key business attributes | Female, pregnancy number (parity sequence), confirming check, linked breeding, expected calving date and basis, closed time, closing reason.
Business identity | At most one OPEN episode per female.
Status / lifecycle | OPEN → CALVED, LOST or DAM_EXITED; VOIDED when its confirming check is voided without dependencies.
Detail – Pregnancy Update | An observation, complication or loss recorded during a pregnancy.
[/TABLE]
[P239 ] Entity: Calving Event
[TABLE block 240]
Field | Value
Purpose | An actual birth of a dam, saved with all its calf outcomes.
Key business attributes | Dam, linked episode (optional), birth time, birth attendant, calving course, dam condition, retained placenta.
Business identity | One calving per dam per birth; a second calving within 30 days is flagged.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED; a correction keeps the record VALID with a "Corrected" marker.
[/TABLE]
[P242 ] Entity: Calf Outcome
[TABLE block 243]
Field | Value
Purpose | The result for one calf of a calving, including the draft data of a live calf.
Key business attributes | Calving, calf number, outcome, sex, birth weight, unit and basis, ear-tag/QR code, proposed pen, breed, registration choice, registration state, registered cattle.
Business identity | Calf number is unique within the calving; ear-tag/QR code is unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | LIVE: PENDING_CONFIRMATION → REGISTERED, or DIED_BEFORE_REGISTRATION; Register now goes directly to REGISTERED.
[/TABLE]
[P245 4] F. Feed Management
[P246 ] Entity: Feed Item
[TABLE block 247]
Field | Value
Purpose | A feed or supplement the farm stocks and feeds.
Key business attributes | Code, name, feed group, stock unit (kg), purchase unit and kg per purchase unit, minimum stock level, nutrition note.
Business identity | Code TA-nn, unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | ACTIVE ⇄ INACTIVE; code and stock unit cannot change after the first stock movement; a feed with movements is never deleted.
[/TABLE]
[P249 ] Entity: Feed Receipt
[TABLE block 250]
Field | Value
Purpose | One delivery of feed from a supplier that adds stock.
Key business attributes | Receipt code, receipt date, supplier (trading partner), invoice or delivery-note number, receiver, storage location, goods amount, transport fee, total payable, amount paid, outstanding amount, payment due date.
Business identity | Code PNK-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → POSTED → VOIDED; a DRAFT changes no stock; a POSTED receipt is voided only while the stock still covers every line.
Detail – Feed Receipt Line | One feed received on a feed receipt.
[/TABLE]
[P252 ] Entity: Stock Adjustment
[TABLE block 253]
Field | Value
Purpose | A stock count or loss record that aligns system stock with counted stock.
Key business attributes | Adjustment code, count date, adjustment type, total difference in kg and value.
Business identity | Code ĐCK-YYYY-nnnn, unique within the farm.
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → POSTED.
Detail – Stock Adjustment Line | The count result of one feed item on a stock adjustment.
[/TABLE]
[P255 ] Entity: Ration
[TABLE block 256]
Field | Value
Purpose | A planned feeding for one or more pens, kept as versions.
Key business attributes | Ration code, name, version number, herd group, pens, sessions per day and times, effective from, effective to, basis.
Business identity | Code + version unique within the farm; at most one version is effective per pen and date.
Status / lifecycle | SCHEDULED → EFFECTIVE → ENDED.
Detail – Ration Component | The quantity of one feed in one ration version.
[/TABLE]
[P258 ] Entity: Feeding Record
[TABLE block 259]
Field | Value
Purpose | One feeding session of one pen on one date.
Key business attributes | Pen, feeding date, session, feeding time, ration version (optional), headcount, planned and actual kg, leftover kg, fed by.
Business identity | Pen + date + session unique among VALID records.
Status / lifecycle | VALID → VOIDED (with reason).
Detail – Feeding Record Line | The planned and actual quantity of one feed in one feeding record.
[/TABLE]
[P261 4] G. Farm Announcements
[P262 ] Entity: Farm Announcement
[TABLE block 263]
Field | Value
Purpose | An administrative message from the Owner or a Manager to members of the farm.
Key business attributes | Farm, author membership, intended audience, state, current published version.
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → PUBLISHED.
Detail – Announcement Version | One immutable published content version.
Detail – Announcement Delivery | The delivery of one version to one recipient, fixed at publication.
[/TABLE]
[P265 4] H. Subscription and Platform Finance
[P266 ] Entity: Plan Price Version
[TABLE block 267]
Field | Value
Purpose | An immutable published price list of the single BeefCare plan.
Key business attributes | Version number, price for 1, 2 and 3 years (VND), supported terms, effective from.
Business identity | Version number is unique; exactly one version is effective at any instant.
Status / lifecycle | SCHEDULED → EFFECTIVE → SUPERSEDED, or SCHEDULED → WITHDRAWN before it takes effect; never edited after publication.
[/TABLE]
[P269 ] Entity: Subscription
[TABLE block 270]
Field | Value
Purpose | The billing relationship of one farm with BeefCare and its current coverage.
Key business attributes | Farm, current period (derived), future periods (derived), latest confirmed end, renewal opening date (derived), anniversary anchor.
Business identity | At most one subscription per farm.
[/TABLE]
[P272 ] Entity: Payment Request
[TABLE block 273]
Field | Value
Purpose | One Owner-initiated quote for a 1-, 2- or 3-year purchase with immutable transfer instructions.
Key business attributes | Payment reference, farm, price version, term (years), amount (VND), receiving account, expiry time (24 h), bank-paid time.
Business identity | Payment reference is unique; at most one unexpired PENDING request per farm.
Status / lifecycle | PENDING → PAID, EXPIRED or CANCELLED; EXPIRED → PAID only for evidence paid within the original window; CANCELLED → PAID only for evidence paid before the cancellation time.
Detail – Purchased Period | The service period granted by one accepted payment.
[/TABLE]
[P275 ] Entity: Bank Transfer Evidence
[TABLE block 276]
Field | Value
Purpose | An authenticated bank transaction received from SePay (or looked up by the PM), kept whether or not it matches a payment.
Key business attributes | Provider transaction ID, receiving account, amount, transfer content, payment code, bank-paid time, match and review state, allocated payment.
Business identity | Provider + environment + namespace + provider transaction ID is unique.
Status / lifecycle | RECEIVED → MATCHED (allocated, terminal) or UNMATCHED / NEEDS_REVIEW → ACCEPTED, HELD, REJECTED or LOCKED_FOR_RETURN.
Detail – Evidence Review | A PM decision or handling event on bank evidence.
[/TABLE]
[P278 ] Entity: Manual Finance Record
[TABLE block 279]
Field | Value
Purpose | Platform income or expense other than subscription receipts, recorded by the PM.
Key business attributes | Record code (FR-nnnn), type, category, amount (VND), occurrence date, scope and farm, document number, source and bank transaction reference, replacement record.
Business identity | Record code is unique; a bank transaction is recognized at most once.
Status / lifecycle | DRAFT → POSTED → VOID; a DRAFT may be discarded.
[/TABLE]
[P281 4] I. Customer Support
[P282 ] Entity: Support Ticket
[TABLE block 283]
Field | Value
Purpose | An Owner's support case about their account or one owned farm.
Key business attributes | Ticket code (TK-nnnn), requester, related farm (optional), category, priority, subject, assigned CS, latest resolution version, current acknowledgment.
Business identity | Ticket code is unique; the requester never changes.
Status / lifecycle | OPEN → IN_PROGRESS ⇄ WAITING_CUSTOMER → RESOLVED → CLOSED; RESOLVED → OPEN on reopen.
Detail – Ticket History Event | The immutable chronology of assignment and status changes of a ticket and its internal requests.
[/TABLE]
[P285 ] Entity: Ticket Message
[TABLE block 286]
Field | Value
Purpose | A PUBLIC reply or a CS-only internal note on a ticket.
Key business attributes | Ticket, author and role, audience, content.
[/TABLE]
[P288 ] Entity: Ticket Resolution
[TABLE block 289]
Field | Value
Purpose | One immutable PUBLIC resolution version recorded by the assigned CS.
Key business attributes | Ticket, version number, resolution text, resolved by.
Business identity | Version number is unique within the ticket.
Detail – Resolution Acknowledgment | The Owner's explicit confirmation that a resolution version solved the issue.
[/TABLE]
[P291 ] Entity: Internal Request
[TABLE block 292]
Field | Value
Purpose | A named request from the assigned CS to a Platform Manager or System Admin.
Key business attributes | Request code (IR-nnn), ticket, recipient (PM or SA), question, outcome, result.
Business identity | At most one OPEN request per ticket and target role.
Status / lifecycle | OPEN → DONE or CANCELLED.
[/TABLE]
[P310 3] 3.3 Data Business Rules
[TABLE block 311]
# | Rule | Description | Enforced On
BR-D01 | Farm-scoped data | Every farm-owned record belongs to one farm; links between records stay within that farm. | Every save and read
BR-D02 | Unique account email | One account per verified email (case-insensitive). | Registration, sign-in
BR-D03 | Google identity binding | A Google identity belongs to one account and is never re-linked. | Google sign-in
BR-D04 | Membership uniqueness | One membership per account and farm; one Owner per farm. | Farm registration, invitation, role change
BR-D05 | Single pending invitation | At most one PENDING invitation per farm and email. | Invitation send
BR-D06 | Platform role uniqueness | At most one ACTIVE platform role per account; at least one ACTIVE System Admin. | Staff access change
BR-D07 | Permanent cattle identity | Internal Cattle ID and display code never change and are never reused. | Herd entry
BR-D08 | Tag uniqueness | QR tag code is unique, one ACTIVE tag per cattle; ear tag number is unique among ACTIVE cattle of the farm. | Tag issue, profile update, herd entry, calving
BR-D09 | Single current pen | One current pen per cattle; a pen never exceeds its capacity. | Herd entry, pen move, exit
BR-D10 | One entry per cattle | One herd entry per cattle; a live calf becomes at most one cattle. | Herd entry
BR-D11 | Unique codes | Pen and catalogue codes are unique within the farm. | Create, update
BR-D12 | No deletion of referenced data | Referenced records are deactivated or kept, never deleted. | Delete, deactivate
BR-D13 | Event time | Occurrence time is kept apart from entry time, not in the future and not before herd entry (except history declared at entry). | Event save, correction
BR-D14 | Corrections keep history | Corrections and voids keep the original values with reason, actor and time. | Correction, void
BR-D15 | Growth content | A growth record has a weight (> 0 kg), a BCS or both. | Growth record
BR-D16 | Dose uniqueness | One scheduled dose per schedule, cattle and dose number, fulfilled by at most one on-farm vaccination. | Schedule, vaccination
BR-D17 | Single open pregnancy | At most one OPEN pregnancy per female, opened only by a PREGNANT check. | Pregnancy check, calving
BR-D18 | Calving content | A calving has at least one calf outcome. | Calving record
BR-D19 | Locked cattle | A draft calf or an EXITED cattle accepts no new events. | Event save
BR-D20 | Announcement immutability | Published versions are immutable. | Publish, correction
BR-D21 | Subscription per farm | One subscription and at most one unexpired PENDING payment request per farm; payment references are unique. | Payment request
BR-D22 | Single use of bank evidence | Each bank transaction is stored once and funds at most one payment request; a PAID request has one evidence and one purchased period. | Payment confirmation, PM review
BR-D23 | Immutable periods and prices | Purchased periods and published prices are never edited; each price version has positive prices for 1, 2 and 3 years. | Confirmation, price publication
BR-D24 | Ticket integrity | The requester never changes; at most one OPEN internal request per ticket and role; resolution versions are immutable. | Ticket commands
BR-D25 | Finance record content | Amounts are positive whole VND; FARM scope needs a farm; POSTED records are immutable. | Finance record
BR-D26 | Single recognition of money | A bank transaction counts once: as a subscription receipt or as manual income. | Payment acceptance, finance record
BR-D27 | Fixed purpose type | A purpose type cannot change once the purpose is used. | Purpose update
BR-D28 | Health case outcome | A CLOSED case has one outcome; OPEN and CHRONIC cases have none. | Case close, reopen
BR-D29 | Unique voucher codes | Voucher codes (PN, PX, PNK, ĐCK) are unique per farm, sequential per year and never reused; a posted voucher has at least one line. | Voucher save
BR-D30 | One open exit per cattle | A cattle is on at most one DRAFT, PENDING or CONFIRMED exit line; only ACTIVE cattle can be added. | Exit voucher
BR-D31 | Non-negative feed stock | Feed stock never becomes negative. | Receipt void, adjustment, feeding
BR-D32 | One feeding record per session | One feeding record per pen, date and session, not in the future. | Feeding record
BR-D33 | One effective ration per pen | At most one effective ration per pen and date. | Ration save
BR-D34 | Payments within total | Payments are positive whole VND and never exceed the voucher total; purchase line prices are greater than 0. | Voucher save, payment
[/TABLE]
[P313 2] 4. User Requirements
[P314 3] 4.1 Actors
[TABLE block 315]
# | Actor | Description
1 | Guest | Unauthenticated visitor. Views the public landing page, registers as a Farm Owner, signs in and resets a forgotten password.
2 | Farm Owner | Account holder who owns one or more farms. In each owned farm: manages farm information, members, the subscription and payments; changes production purposes; records, confirms and voids herd exit vouchers and collections; submits support tickets. Has every permission of a Farm Manager.
3 | Farm Manager | Farm member with the Manager role. Records and corrects care, reproduction and feeding; maintains pens, cattle profiles (except production purpose), QR tags, herd entry vouchers and payments, catalogues, parameters and feed; writes announcements to Technicians; uses the dashboard and reports. Cannot manage members, the subscription or herd exits.
4 | Farm Technician | Farm member with the Technician role; read-only. Scans QR tags and views cattle, pens, care, today's rations, the feeding log, catalogues, reminders and received announcements.
5 | Customer Support (CS) | Platform staff. Reads the support queue; the assigned CS claims, processes, replies, writes internal notes, resolves and closes tickets and sends internal requests to a named PM or SA. Has no farm operational access.
6 | Platform Manager (PM) | Platform staff. Views farm service status and suspends or restores farm service; views subscriptions, payments and bank evidence and reconciles payments; publishes plan prices; manages manual finance records and the platform cash report; handles internal requests assigned to them. Has no access to cattle or care data.
7 | System Admin (SA) | Platform staff. Views and suspends or restores user accounts, grants and revokes CS, PM and SA access, reviews the platform audit history and handles internal requests assigned to them. Keeps at least one ACTIVE System Admin.
8 | Google Identity Provider | External system that authenticates a person through OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect and returns the verified identity.
9 | SePay | External system that reports bank transactions on the BeefCare receiving account through authenticated webhooks, provides transaction lookup and renders VietQR images.
10 | Email Delivery Service | External system that delivers transactional emails (invitations, verification and reset links, notices).
[/TABLE]
[P317 3] 4.2 Use Cases

UC-FE01-03 | Sign Out | A signed-in user ends the current session.
UC-FE01-04 | View and Update Own Profile | A signed-in user views own profile and roles, and updates name and contact phone.
UC-FE01-05 | Reset Forgotten Password | A Guest resets a forgotten password through a link sent by email.
UC-FE01-06 | Change Password | A signed-in user changes the password, or sets a first password for a Google-only account.
[/TABLE]

UC-FE03-03 | Accept Farm Invitation | The invited person accepts the invitation and becomes a member of the farm.
UC-FE03-04 | Manage Farm Members | The Farm Owner views the members of the farm and changes a role, or deactivates or reactivates a member; a Manager only views the list.
UC-FE03-05 | Manage Platform User Accounts | The System Admin searches user accounts and suspends or restores an account.
