[P437 3] 5.2 External API Inventory
[TABLE block 438]
# | API Name | Calling Path | Description
API-01 | VietQR image | BeefCare → SePay QR service | Renders the VietQR image of a payment request from the receiving bank, account number, amount and payment reference.
API-02 | SePay transaction webhook | SePay → BeefCare | Delivers each bank transaction on the BeefCare receiving account; BeefCare authenticates it, stores the evidence and runs payment confirmation.
API-03 | SePay transaction lookup | BeefCare → SePay | Lets the PM reconciliation workbench retrieve transactions of the receiving account for a period or transaction ID.
API-04 | Transactional email | BeefCare → Email Delivery Service | Sends invitation, email-verification, password-reset, password-changed, subscription and support notification emails.
API-05 | Google sign-in (OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect) | BeefCare ↔ Google Identity Provider | Redirects the user to Google and validates the returned identity (subject, verified email, name) for registration, sign-in and invitation acceptance.
[/TABLE]
[P440 3] 5.3 Background Job Inventory
[TABLE block 441]
# | Job Name | Group | Description
JOB-01 | Evaluate farm reminders | Scheduled + event | Every 15 minutes and after each saved source change: creates, updates, resolves or cancels reminders REM-01 to REM-13.
JOB-02 | Expire farm invitations | Scheduled | Every 15 minutes: marks PENDING invitations past their expiry time as EXPIRED.
JOB-03 | Expire unpaid payment requests | Scheduled | Every 5 minutes: marks PENDING payment requests past their 24-hour expiry as EXPIRED.
JOB-04 | Evaluate subscription coverage | Scheduled | Every 15 minutes: recalculates farm service status when a purchased period starts or ends.
JOB-05 | Generate subscription notices | Scheduled | Daily at 07:00 (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh): creates renewal-opening, expiry-approaching and expired notices for Owners.
JOB-06 | Process received bank evidence | Queue | Continuously: processes stored SePay evidence through payment confirmation; retries unfinished work.
JOB-07 | Dispatch notifications and emails | Queue | Continuously: delivers committed in-app notifications and emails with retry and deduplication.
JOB-08 | Purge expired tokens and snapshots | Scheduled | Daily at 02:00: removes expired verification, reset and invitation link tokens and expired report snapshots.
[/TABLE]
[P443 1] II. Use Case Specifications
[P444 2] 1. FE-01 Authentication and Account Access
[P445 3] 1.1 Register Farm Owner Account
[TABLE block 446]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE01-01 — Register Farm Owner Account
Created By | LamKD | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Guest | Secondary Actors | Google Identity Provider; Email Delivery Service
Trigger | The Guest selects "Register as Farm Owner" on the landing page or the sign-in page.
Description | As a farm owner, I want to create a BeefCare account with my Google account or with my email and a password, so that I can register and manage my farms.
Preconditions | The user is not signed in.
Postconditions | An ACTIVE account with a verified email exists and the user is signed in. / Owner registration is complete and Register Farm is shown.
Normal Flow | 1. Guest selects Continue with Google on Register Farm Owner. / 2. System redirects the Guest to Google. / 3. Guest signs in and gives consent at Google. / 4. System validates the Google identity and creates the account, or reuses the account with the same email. / 5. System signs the user in and shows Complete Owner Profile with the verified email. / 6. Guest confirms the full name, enters an optional phone, accepts the terms and submits. / 7. System saves the Owner profile and opens Register Farm.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Register with email and password (step 1) / 1. Guest enters full name, email and password, accepts the terms and selects Create account. / 2. System creates the account pending verification and emails a verification link valid for 24 hours. / 3. Guest opens the link. / 4. System activates the account and signs the user in. / 5. Return to step 5. / AF2. Account already exists (step 4) / 1. System reuses the existing account. / 2. If Owner registration is complete, System opens Select Farm and the use case ends. Otherwise return to step 5. / AF3. Resend verification email (AF1 step 2) / 1. Guest selects Resend. / 2. System invalidates the previous link and sends a new one, at most 5 times per hour.
Exceptions | E1. Google sign-in is cancelled: no account is created. / E2. The email in AF1 already has an account: System links to Sign In and Forgot Password; no second account is created. / E3. A field is invalid: System shows the error under the field. / E4. The verification link is expired or used: System offers Resend. / E5. The account is SUSPENDED: System shows Account Suspended; no session is created. / E6. Sending the email or saving fails: nothing is saved.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Once per Farm Owner.
Business Rules | GBR-01, GBR-02, GBR-03, GBR-05; BR-D02, BR-D03. Owner registration grants no farm, role or service. Phone is contact information only.
Other Information | API-04, API-05. Related: UC-FE01-02, UC-FE02-01.
Assumptions | Google manages its own authentication, security and recovery; BeefCare never receives a Google password.
[/TABLE]
[P448 3] 1.2 Sign In
[TABLE block 449]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE01-02 — Sign In
Created By | LamKD | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Guest | Secondary Actors | Google Identity Provider
Trigger | The Guest selects "Sign in", or opens a protected page, a notification link or an invitation link while signed out.
Description | As a registered user, I want to sign in with Google or with my email and password, so that I can work in the farms and platform areas I am authorized for.
Preconditions | The user has an ACTIVE BeefCare account and is not signed in.
Postconditions | The user is signed in and sees only the farms and platform areas the user is authorized for.
Normal Flow | 1. Guest enters email and password and selects Sign in. / 2. System checks the account, the password and that sign-in is not locked. / 3. System creates the session, for 8 hours of inactivity or 30 days with Remember me. / 4. System loads the platform role and the ACTIVE farm memberships of the user. / 5. System opens the home screen of the role, the only available farm, or Select Farm when there are several farms.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Continue with Google (step 1) / 1. Guest selects Continue with Google and signs in at Google. / 2. System finds the account by the Google identity or creates a new account. / 3. Return to step 3. / AF2. Owner registration not completed (step 5) / 1. System opens Complete Owner Profile. / AF3. No farm and no platform role (step 5) / 1. System shows a message with Register as Farm Owner and guidance to open the invitation email. / AF4. Started from an invitation link (step 5) / 1. System continues with Accept Farm Invitation.
Exceptions | E1. The email or password is wrong: System shows a generic error and counts the failed attempt. / E2. Five consecutive failures: password sign-in is locked for 15 minutes; Google sign-in stays available. / E3. The account is SUSPENDED: System shows Account Suspended with the official contact; no session is created. / E4. Google sign-in is cancelled or fails: no session is created. / E5. The requested farm is no longer authorized: System shows the remaining authorized farms. / E6. The service is unavailable: no session is created.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Several times a day per user.
Business Rules | GBR-01, GBR-02, GBR-04, GBR-09, GBR-14.
Other Information | API-05. Related: UC-FE01-01, UC-FE01-05, UC-FE02-02, UC-FE03-03.
Assumptions | Session duration values are configuration defaults.
[/TABLE]
[P451 3] 1.3 Reset Forgotten Password
[TABLE block 452]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE01-05 — Reset Forgotten Password
Created By | LamKD | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Guest | Secondary Actors | Email Delivery Service
Trigger | The Guest selects "Forgot password?" on Sign In.
Description | As a user who forgot my password, I want to receive a single-use link by email and set a new password, so that I can sign in again.
Preconditions | The user is not signed in.
Postconditions | The new password is saved and all sessions of the account are ended.
Normal Flow | 1. Guest enters the email on Forgot Password and selects Send reset link. / 2. System shows the same confirmation whether or not the email exists. / 3. System emails a reset link valid for 30 minutes when the account has a password. / 4. Guest opens the link and enters the new password twice. / 5. System checks the link, saves the new password, ends all sessions and emails a confirmation. / 6. System opens Sign In with a success message.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Account uses Google only (step 3) / 1. System emails advice to sign in with Google. No password is created. / 2. The use case ends. / AF2. Link expired or already used (step 5) / 1. System shows that the link is no longer valid and offers to send a new one.
Exceptions | E1. The link is expired or used: System shows Link no longer valid with Send new link. / E2. The password breaks the rules or the confirmation differs: the old password stays. / E3. The account is SUSPENDED: the password is reset but sign-in stays blocked. / E4. Sending the email or saving fails: the old password stays.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-02, GBR-03, GBR-04. The response never reveals whether an email is registered.
Other Information | API-04.
Assumptions | The user has access to the mailbox of the account email.
[/TABLE]
[P454 3] 1.4 Change Password
[TABLE block 455]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE01-06 — Change Password
Created By | LamKD | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager, Farm Technician, Customer Support, Platform Manager, System Admin | Secondary Actors | Google Identity Provider (re-authentication); Email Delivery Service
Trigger | The user selects "Change password" or "Add password" in Settings (SCR-08).
Description | As a signed-in user, I want to change my password, or add a first password to my Google-only account, so that I can also sign in with email and password.
Preconditions | The user is signed in.
Postconditions | The new password is saved and the other sessions are ended.
Normal Flow | 1. User selects Change password. / 2. User enters the current password and the new password twice and selects Save. / 3. System checks the current password and validates the new password. / 4. System saves the new password, ends the other sessions and emails a confirmation. / 5. System shows a success message.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Add a first password (step 1) / 1. System asks the user to sign in with Google again. / 2. User enters the new password twice and selects Save. / 3. Return to step 4.
Exceptions | E1. The current password is wrong: System shows an error; after five failures password sign-in is locked for 15 minutes. / E2. The new password breaks the rules or the confirmation differs: nothing is saved. / E3. The session has expired: the user signs in again; nothing is saved. / E4. Saving fails: the previous password stays.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-02. Passwords are never displayed, logged or returned.
Other Information | API-04, API-05.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P457 2] 2. FE-02 Farm Workspace and Service Management
[P458 3] 2.1 Register Farm Workspace
[TABLE block 459]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE02-01 — Register Farm Workspace
Created By | DucNV | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Owner completes the Owner profile (UC-FE01-01) or selects "Register new farm" on Select Farm.
Description | As a farm owner, I want to register a farm, so that I can subscribe it and manage its herd separately from my other farms.
Preconditions | The Farm Owner is signed in and the Owner profile is complete.
Postconditions | A farm in PENDING_PAYMENT exists with the Farm Owner as its owner, the default parameters and the starter catalogue.
Normal Flow | 1. System shows Register Farm. / 2. Farm Owner enters the farm name, address, contact data, expected herd size and main breeds and selects Create farm and continue. / 3. System validates the data. / 4. System creates the farm, the Owner membership, the default parameters and the starter catalogue. / 5. System opens Select Term and Review for the new farm.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Choose a plan later (step 5) / 1. Farm Owner selects Later. / 2. System keeps the farm in PENDING_PAYMENT and shows it on Select Farm with Pay to activate.
Exceptions | E1. Owner registration is not complete: System opens Complete Owner Profile; no farm is created. / E2. A field is missing or invalid: nothing is saved. / E3. Saving fails: no farm is created.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Once per farm.
Business Rules | GBR-05, GBR-12, GBR-13; BR-D04. One registration request creates at most one farm.
Other Information | Related: UC-FE19-02.
Assumptions | Farm names need not be unique.
[/TABLE]
[P461 3] 2.2 View and Select Authorized Farm Workspace
[TABLE block 462]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE02-02 — View and Select Authorized Farm Workspace
Created By | DucNV | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager, Farm Technician | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user signs in with more than one farm, or opens the farm switcher.
Description | As a farm member, I want to see my farms with my role and their service status and choose one, so that I work only with that farm's data.
Preconditions | The user is signed in.
Postconditions | The selected farm is the current farm with the role of the user in it.
Normal Flow | 1. User opens Select Farm. / 2. System shows each farm of the user with its service status, the role of the user, the herd size and the service end date. / 3. User selects Enter farm on an ACTIVE farm. / 4. System checks the membership and the service status. / 5. System sets the current farm and opens Farm Dashboard, or Technician Home for a Farm Technician.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Farm not ACTIVE, Farm Owner (step 3) / 1. Farm Owner selects Pay, Renew or Send support ticket on the farm card. / 2. System opens the matching screen. Only farm information, subscription and support are available. / AF2. Farm not ACTIVE, Farm Manager or Farm Technician (step 2) / 1. System shows the farm card disabled with a message. / AF3. Only one available farm (step 1) / 1. System opens the farm directly.
Exceptions | E1. A membership is deactivated or its role changes meanwhile: System refreshes the list. / E2. Loading fails: System shows an error.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | At every sign-in; several times a day for multi-farm users.
Business Rules | GBR-09, GBR-12, GBR-13, GBR-14.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]
[P464 3] 2.3 Suspend or Restore Farm Service
[TABLE block 465]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE02-05 — Suspend or Restore Farm Service
Created By | DucNV | Date Created | 17/10/2026
Primary Actor | Platform Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The PM selects "Suspend" or "Restore" on a farm in Farm Service Overview.
Description | As a Platform Manager, I want to suspend a farm's service with a reason, or restore it, so that the platform can stop misuse without changing payments.
Preconditions | The farm is not SUSPENDED for a suspension, or SUSPENDED for a restore.
Postconditions | The service status of the farm is changed, the change is kept in the history and the Farm Owner is notified.
Normal Flow | 1. Platform Manager selects Suspend or Restore on a farm. / 2. System shows the farm, its status and its purchased period. / 3. Platform Manager enters the reason and confirms. / 4. System saves the change and sets the new service status. / 5. System notifies the Farm Owner and shows a success message.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Restore without a current period (step 4) / 1. System sets the status to EXPIRED or PENDING_PAYMENT instead of ACTIVE. / 2. Return to step 5.
Exceptions | E1. The user has no ACTIVE PM access: the action is denied. / E2. The reason is empty: nothing is saved. / E3. The farm is already in the requested state: no new record is created. / E4. The farm was changed in another session: System asks the PM to reload. / E5. Saving fails: the status is unchanged. / E6. Sending the notification fails: the change is kept and the notice is retried without duplicates.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Rare.
Business Rules | GBR-11, GBR-13, GBR-15, GBR-86.
Other Information | Related: UC-FE20-11 (when requested by CS).
Assumptions | —

Preconditions | At least one ACTIVE breed, production purpose and pen exist.

Normal Flow | 1. User selects Create entry voucher. / 2. User enters the entry date, the seller and the voucher information. / 3. A calf is already registered or not LIVE: that line is rejected. / E6. The amount paid exceeds the total payable: nothing is saved. / E7. The seller or another required field is missing: nothing is saved. / E8. A catalogue value is inactive: nothing is saved. / E9. Saving fails: nothing is created and the voucher stays DRAFT.

Postconditions | A valid growth record is saved and the weight indicators are recalculated.
Normal Flow | 1. User selects Record weight and BCS. / 2. User enters the time, the method and the weight, the BCS, or both. / 3. User selects Save. / 4. System validates the values. / 5. System saves the record, recalculates the weight and ADG and shows a success message.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Measuring tape (step 2) / 1. User enters heart girth and body length. / 2. System calculates the weight. The user may change it. / 3. Return to step 3. / AF2. Large weight change (step 4) / 1. System asks the user to confirm a change of more than 20 percent. / 2. Return to step 5.
Exceptions | E1. Neither weight nor BCS is entered: nothing is saved. / E2. The weight is not positive: nothing is saved. / E3. The measured time is in the future or before herd entry: nothing is saved. / E4. The cattle has EXITED: the record is read-only. / E5. Saving fails: nothing is saved.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Daily on the farm; monthly per animal.
Business Rules | GBR-08, GBR-26, GBR-38, GBR-39; BR-D13, BR-D15, BR-D19.
Other Information | Related: UC-FE14-01.
Assumptions | The farm BCS scale is configured in UC-FE18-05.
[/TABLE]

Created By | TuanPQ | Date Created | 21/10/2026

[P570 2] 13. FE-13 Herd Exit Recording
[P571 3] 13.1 Record and Confirm Herd Exit Voucher
[TABLE block 572]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE13-01 — Record and Confirm Herd Exit Voucher
Created By | AnNT | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Owner selects "Create exit voucher" or "Continue" on a DRAFT voucher in Herd Exit Vouchers, or "View & confirm" on a PENDING death voucher, the dashboard or a notification.
Description | As a farm owner, I want to record each departure of cattle as one voucher with weights, prices and the money to collect, and confirm it after reviewing open items, so that the herd, the records of each animal and the buyer's balance are correct.
Preconditions | The cattle are ACTIVE and on no other open exit voucher.
Postconditions | The voucher is CONFIRMED and its cattle are EXITED.
Normal Flow | 1. Farm Owner selects Create exit voucher. / 2. Farm Owner selects the exit type and enters the exit date, the buyer and the voucher information. / 3. Farm Owner adds cattle by scanning or selecting them. / 4. Farm Owner enters the exit weights, the prices, the deductions and the amount collected. / 5. System shows the totals and the checks: meat withdrawal, open health cases, future doses, open pregnancy. / 6. Farm Owner confirms the checks and selects Confirm exit. / 7. System confirms the voucher, sets the cattle EXITED, closes their pens and cancels their future doses and reminders. / 8. System opens the voucher detail.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Death or loss voucher (step 1) / 1. Farm Owner opens a pending death voucher, or creates one directly. / 2. Farm Owner enters the cause and the carcass disposal. / 3. Return to step 5. / AF2. Dismiss a pending voucher (step 1) / 1. Farm Owner selects Dismiss and enters the reason. / 2. System dismisses the voucher. The cattle stays ACTIVE. / AF3. Save draft (step 6) / 1. Farm Owner selects Save draft. The cattle stay ACTIVE.
Exceptions | E1. The exit date is invalid: nothing is saved. / E2. A cattle is already on another open voucher: that line is rejected. / E3. The voucher has no cattle line: the voucher cannot be confirmed. / E4. The deposit exceeds the total receivable: nothing is saved. / E5. The type, date, buyer, destination, cause or another required field is missing: nothing is saved. / E6. The confirmation is not ticked: the voucher is not confirmed. / E7. A cattle is no longer ACTIVE or the voucher was changed: System asks the user to reload. / E8. Saving fails: nothing changes.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Weekly.
Business Rules | GBR-07, GBR-34, GBR-35, GBR-46, GBR-91, GBR-92, GBR-94, GBR-95, GBR-97; BR-D09, BR-D30, BR-D29, BR-D30, BR-D34.
Other Information | Related: UC-FE10-03, UC-FE13-04.
Assumptions | Collections from the buyer are recorded in BeefCare as farm bookkeeping; invoices and tax documents are outside BeefCare.
[/TABLE]
[P574 3] 13.2 Void Herd Exit Voucher
[TABLE block 575]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE13-03 — Void Herd Exit Voucher

[P581 2] 15. FE-19 Subscription and Payment Management
[P582 3] 15.1 Request Subscription Payment
[TABLE block 583]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE19-02 — Request Subscription Payment
Created By | DucNV | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner | Secondary Actors | SePay (VietQR image)
Trigger | The Owner selects "Renew plan" on Farm Subscription, "Pay to activate" on Select Farm, or continues from Register Farm (first purchase).
Description | As a farm owner, I want to choose a 1-, 2- or 3-year term and get transfer instructions with VietQR, so that I can pay for my farm by bank transfer.
Preconditions | The farm is not SUSPENDED and a price version is in effect.
Postconditions | A PENDING payment request valid for 24 hours exists and the transfer instructions are shown.
Normal Flow | 1. Farm Owner selects Renew plan or Pay to activate. / 2. System shows the three terms with their prices. / 3. Farm Owner selects a term and reviews the amount and the estimated period. / 4. Farm Owner selects Create payment request. / 5. System checks the renewal window, that no pending request exists and that the price is unchanged. / 6. System creates the request with a unique payment code. / 7. System shows the VietQR code, the bank details and the progress.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Pending request exists (step 5) / 1. System reopens the instructions of the existing request. / AF2. Price changed (step 5) / 1. System shows the new price. / 2. Return to step 3. / AF3. Cancel the request (step 7) / 1. Farm Owner selects Cancel request before any transfer is received. / 2. System sets the request CANCELLED and offers a new request.
Exceptions | E1. The user is not the Owner: the action is denied. / E2. The farm is outside the renewal window: System shows the opening date. / E3. The farm is SUSPENDED: System offers Send support ticket. / E4. Prices or the receiving account are unavailable: System offers Retry. / E5. The VietQR image cannot be shown: the transfer details stay shown as text. / E6. Saving fails: no request is created. / E7. A transfer was already received when cancelling: the request stays PENDING. / E8. The request is no longer an unexpired PENDING request when cancelling: System shows its current status.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Once every 1–3 years per farm.
Business Rules | GBR-69, GBR-70, GBR-71, GBR-72, GBR-75; BR-D21.
Other Information | API-01. JOB-03 expires requests.
Assumptions | The Owner transfers from a Vietnamese bank account that supports VietQR.
[/TABLE]
[P585 3] 15.2 Reconcile Subscription Payment
[TABLE block 586]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE19-03 — Reconcile Subscription Payment
Created By | DucNV | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | Platform Manager | Secondary Actors | SePay (transaction lookup)
Trigger | The PM opens an evidence item marked for review in Payments and Reconciliation, from a reconciliation notification or an internal request.
Description | As a Platform Manager, I want to review payment requests and bank evidence that was not confirmed automatically and accept, hold or reject it with a reason, so that every transfer is handled exactly once.
Preconditions | The bank evidence is authenticated and not yet allocated.
Postconditions | The evidence is accepted and the request is PAID, or the evidence is held or rejected with a reason.
Normal Flow | 1. Platform Manager opens an evidence to review. / 2. System shows the transaction and the candidate requests. / 3. Platform Manager selects the request, chooses Accept and enters the reason. / 4. System checks the amount, the receiving account and the payment window. / 5. System marks the request PAID, creates the purchased period and notifies the Farm Owner.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Hold or reject (step 3) / 1. Platform Manager chooses Hold or Reject and enters the reason. / 2. System saves the decision. No service is granted. / AF2. Lock for return (step 3) / 1. Platform Manager locks the evidence for a return with a reason. / 2. Platform Manager later records the return or unlocks the evidence.
Exceptions | E1. Acceptance is blocked by the amount, account, window, allocation or a manual income conflict: System shows the reason; nothing is allocated. / E2. No candidate is selected for a mistyped reference: nothing is allocated. / E3. The reason is empty: nothing is saved. / E4. The evidence or request was changed meanwhile, for example confirmed by SePay: System asks the PM to reload. / E5. Saving fails: nothing changes.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Daily or as needed.
Business Rules | GBR-15, GBR-72, GBR-73, GBR-74, GBR-84, GBR-86; BR-D22, BR-D26.
Other Information | API-03. Related: UC-FE19-05, UC-FE20-11.

[P591 3] 15.4 Confirm Subscription Payment from SePay
[TABLE block 592]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE19-05 — Confirm Subscription Payment from SePay
Created By | DucNV | Date Created | 21/10/2026
Primary Actor | SePay | Secondary Actors | Email Delivery Service
Trigger | SePay calls the BeefCare webhook (API-02) for a transaction on the receiving account.
Description | As the platform, I want to authenticate each bank transaction reported by SePay and confirm the matching payment request exactly once, so that farms are activated automatically and safely.
Preconditions | The SePay webhook is configured.
Postconditions | The transaction is stored once. When it matches, the request is PAID and the period is created.
Normal Flow | 1. SePay sends a bank transaction. / 2. System verifies the signature and stores the transaction once. / 3. System matches the payment code, the amount and the payment window with one PENDING request. / 4. System marks the request PAID, creates the purchased period and updates the service status. / 5. System notifies the Farm Owner.
Alternative Flows | AF1. No match (step 3) / 1. System marks the transaction for review by the Platform Manager. / AF2. Farm suspended (step 4) / 1. System creates the period but the farm stays SUSPENDED.
Exceptions | E1. The signature or timestamp is invalid: System replies HTTP 401 and keeps only restricted delivery metadata. / E2. Storing the evidence fails before step 3 completes: System sends no success reply, so SePay retries. / E3. Processing fails after storage: the background job retries; the evidence is kept.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Every subscription transfer.
Business Rules | GBR-13, GBR-15, GBR-72, GBR-73, GBR-74, GBR-84; BR-D21, BR-D22.
Other Information | API-02, JOB-06, JOB-07. Related: UC-FE19-03, UC-FE21-01.
Assumptions | SePay retries failed deliveries; the same transaction ID is kept across retries.
[/TABLE]

Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The user selects "Create ration" or "Edit" on a ration in Rations.
Description | As a farm owner or manager, I want to define how much of each feed every animal of a pen should get per session, and change it from a chosen date, so that daily feeding follows a plan and past feeding records stay unchanged.
Preconditions | ACTIVE feed items and pens exist.
Postconditions | A ration version is saved and applies to its pens from the effective date.
Normal Flow | 1. User selects Create ration or Edit. / 2. User enters the name, the pens and the effective date. / 3. User sets the sessions per day and the kg per head of each feed. / 4. System shows the quantity and cost per day and the checks. / 5. User selects Save ration. / 6. System saves the version and ends the previous version the day before.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Stop a ration (step 1) / 1. User enters an end date. The pens have no ration after it.
Exceptions | E1. The effective date is earlier than tomorrow: nothing is saved. / E2. A pen has a scheduled version of another ration on that date: nothing is saved. / E3. No component is added or a quantity is not positive: nothing is saved. / E4. The ration was changed in another session: System asks the user to reload. / E5. Saving fails: nothing changes.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Monthly per ration.
Business Rules | GBR-101, GBR-103, GBR-105; BR-D33.
Other Information | Related: UC-FE23-05, UC-FE23-08.
Assumptions | Rations are expressed per head for all animals of the pen; individual feeding is not planned.
[/TABLE]
[P639 3] 18.3 Record Feeding Session
[TABLE block 640]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE23-08 — Record Feeding Session
Created By | TaiDX | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Owner or a Manager selects "Record feeding session" or an empty session cell on Feeding Log, or opens reminder REM-11.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to record the feed actually given to a pen in each session, so that stock is deducted automatically and consumption and cost can be compared with the ration.
Preconditions | The pen is ACTIVE.
Postconditions | The feeding session is saved and the stock of each feed is reduced.
Normal Flow | 1. User selects Record feeding session. / 2. User selects the pen and the session. / 3. System shows the planned quantity of each feed and the stock. / 4. User enters the actual quantities and selects Save. / 5. System checks that the session is not recorded yet and that the stock is enough. / 6. System saves the record and reduces the stock.
Alternative Flows | AF1. No effective ration (step 3) / 1. User enters the feeds without a plan. / 2. Return to step 4.
Exceptions | E1. A record already exists for the pen, date and session: nothing is saved. / E2. An actual quantity exceeds the stock: nothing is saved. / E3. The date is in the future: nothing is saved. / E4. The pen, session or quantities are missing: nothing is saved. / E5. Saving fails: no stock is deducted.
Priority | High
Frequency of Use | Two to three times a day per pen.
Business Rules | GBR-102, GBR-103, GBR-104, GBR-105; BR-D31, BR-D32.
Other Information | Medicine mixed into feed is recorded as a treatment in FE-10, not here.
Assumptions | Feeding is recorded per pen, not per animal.
[/TABLE]
[P642 3] 18.4 Correct or Void Feeding Record
[TABLE block 643]
UC ID and Name | UC-FE23-09 — Correct or Void Feeding Record
Created By | TaiDX | Date Created | 22/10/2026
Primary Actor | Farm Owner, Farm Manager | Secondary Actors | None
Trigger | The Owner or a Manager selects "Correct" or "Void" on a recorded session in Feeding Log.
Description | As a farm manager, I want to correct or void a wrong feeding record with a reason, so that stock and consumption are right while the original entry stays traceable.
Preconditions | The feeding record is VALID.
Postconditions | The record has the new quantities and the stock is adjusted, or the record is voided and the stock is returned.
Normal Flow | 1. User selects Correct on a feeding record. / 2. User enters the new quantities and the reason. / 3. System shows the stock difference of each feed. / 4. User selects Save. / 5. System saves the correction and adjusts the stock.
Alternative Flows | AF1. Void the record (step 1) / 1. User selects Void record and enters the reason. / 2. System voids the record and returns the stock.
Exceptions | E1. The stock would become negative: nothing is saved. / E2. The reason is empty: nothing is saved. / E3. The record was changed in another session: System asks the user to reload. / E4. Saving fails: nothing changes.
Priority | Medium
Frequency of Use | Weekly.
Business Rules | GBR-102, GBR-103; BR-D14, BR-D31.
Assumptions | —
[/TABLE]

Quarantine certificate | Text, max 50 characters. A warning is shown when empty.
Vehicle plate | Text, max 20 characters.
Receiving note | Long text, max 1,000 characters.
Cattle in the voucher

Cattle lines | Ear tag, breed, sex, life stage, purpose, age, weight, receiving pen, unit price and line amount.
Attachments
Files | PDF or JPG, max 10 MB each.
Payment

Transport fee | Whole VND, 0 or more.
Total payable | Read-only cattle amount plus transport fee.
Amount paid | Whole VND, 0 or more, not above the total payable.
Outstanding | Read-only total payable minus amount paid.
Payment method | Cash or bank transfer. Required when an amount is paid.
Balance due date | Date.
Checks before saving

Ear tag number | Text, max 30 characters, unique. Empty means tag later.

Colour and markings | Text, max 255 characters.
Breed | Required. ACTIVE breed.
Sex | Required. Male or Female.
Birth month | Year and month, estimated. Empty means unknown.
Life stage | Required. Proposed from the age at entry.
Production purpose | Required. ACTIVE purpose valid for the sex.
Entry weight | Number 1 to 1,500 kg. Required for per kg pricing.
Weight basis | Required. Measured or Estimated.
Receiving pen | Required. ACTIVE pen with free places. A warning reason is needed when a criterion is not met.
Unit price or line amount | Required. Greater than 0.
Quarantine until | Date not before the entry date.
Vaccination history | Required. Known, None or Unknown, with vaccine and date when known.
Known diseases | Required. Known, None or Unknown, with a description when known.
Reproduction history | Required for females. Known, None or Unknown, with earlier calvings, pregnancy at entry and months pregnant.
Import Cattle from Excel dialog
File | Required. XLSX from the template, max 200 rows and 5 MB.
Check result | Read-only result per row: valid, warning or error with the reason. Rows with errors are not added.
[/TABLE]

Summary tiles | Feeds in use, low or out of stock, stock value, average use over 7 days.
Search, group and status | Filters. Status Sufficient, Low or Out of stock.
Stock list
Feed | Code, name and group.
Current stock | kg, with the equivalent in purchase units.
Minimum level | kg.
Average per day | kg over 7 days.
Days of cover | Current stock divided by the average per day.
Lot near expiry | Lot and expiry date within 30 days.
Status | Sufficient, Low or Out of stock.
[/TABLE]

[P1533 2] 24. Background Job Inventory
[P1534 3] 24.1 Evaluate Farm Reminders (JOB-01)
[P1535 ] Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes, and event-driven after each committed change of a source record (weight, estrus, breeding, check, calving, dose, schedule, pen criteria, profile, parameters, feed stock movement, ration, feeding record).
[P1536 ] Purpose: Keep the farm reminders REM-01 to REM-13 consistent with the source records and the effective parameters (Table V-1, GBR-63).
[P1537 ] Processing steps:
[P1538 ] 1. Collect the farms and sources to evaluate: the changed sources for an event run, all ACTIVE farms for a scheduled run.
[P1539 ] 2. Apply parameter versions whose effective time has been reached and load the effective version of each farm.
[P1540 ] 3. For each rule, find the candidate sources and calculate the due date or window; for REM-08 compare each ACTIVE cattle with the criteria of its current pen, and its age (from the birth date) with the life-stage ages, and check first calvings of females not yet Adult; for REM-10 compare the stock of each ACTIVE feed item with its minimum level; for REM-11 compare the sessions of each effective ration whose time + 2 hours has passed today with the feeding records.
[P1541 ] 4. Create or update each reminder by (rule, source, occurrence key); unchanged reminders are not rewritten.
[P1542 ] 5. Resolve, cancel or supersede reminders whose condition no longer holds.
[P1543 ] 6. Log the number of created, updated, resolved and cancelled reminders per farm.
[P1544 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1545]
Scenario | System behavior
Database unreachable | Retries 3 times at 1-minute intervals, then logs an error. The next run recomputes everything; reminders are idempotent.
Parameter missing for a rule | The rule is skipped for that farm; screens show MSG-FE14-01. Other rules continue.
Error in one farm | The farm is skipped and logged; other farms are processed.
[/TABLE]
[P1547 3] 24.2 Expire Farm Invitations (JOB-02)
[P1548 ] Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes.
[P1549 ] Purpose: Mark invitations that passed their validity as EXPIRED (GBR-16).
[P1550 ] Processing steps:
[P1551 ] 1. Find PENDING invitations whose expiry time is before now.
[P1552 ] 2. Set them EXPIRED and save the history.
[P1553 ] 3. Log the number of expired invitations.
[P1554 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1555]
Scenario | System behavior
Job fails or is delayed | Acceptance and resend check the expiry time in real time, so an expired link never grants access. The next run expires the remaining invitations.
No invitation to expire | The job ends without action.
[/TABLE]
[P1557 3] 24.3 Expire Unpaid Payment Requests (JOB-03)
[P1558 ] Trigger: Scheduled every 5 minutes.
[P1559 ] Purpose: Mark PENDING payment requests older than 24 hours as EXPIRED and inform the Owner (GBR-70).
[P1560 ] Processing steps:
[P1561 ] 1. Find PENDING requests whose expiry time is before now.
[P1562 ] 2. Set them EXPIRED (never a PAID request) and create the Owner notification.
[P1563 ] 3. Log the number of expired requests.
[P1564 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1565]
Scenario | System behavior
Job fails or is delayed | Request creation and confirmation check the expiry in real time. Evidence paid inside the original window still confirms the request (UC-FE19-05 AF3).
Concurrent confirmation | The confirmation and the expiry are serialized per farm; a PAID request is never set EXPIRED.
[/TABLE]
[P1567 3] 24.4 Evaluate Subscription Coverage (JOB-04)
[P1568 ] Trigger: Scheduled every 15 minutes.
[P1569 ] Purpose: Update the farm service status when a purchased period starts or ends (GBR-13).
[P1570 ] Processing steps:
[P1571 ] 1. Find farms with a purchased period that started or ended since the previous run.
[P1572 ] 2. Recalculate the service status: ACTIVE when a period covers now, EXPIRED when coverage has ended, PENDING_PAYMENT when no period was ever accepted; SUSPENDED is never changed by the job.
[P1573 ] 3. Save the status change and audit entry; create an "expired" notification for the Owner.
[P1574 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1575]
Scenario | System behavior
Job fails or is delayed | Every request derives the service status from the periods in real time, so access is always correct; only notifications are delayed.
Database unreachable | Retries at the next run.
[/TABLE]
[P1577 3] 24.5 Generate Subscription Notices (JOB-05)
[P1578 ] Trigger: Scheduled daily at 07:00 (Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh).
[P1579 ] Purpose: Inform Owners about renewal and expiry of each farm's coverage.
[P1580 ] Processing steps:
[P1581 ] 1. For each farm with a confirmed end date, determine whether today is the renewal opening day (end − 90 days), 30, 7 or 1 day before the end, or the day coverage expired.
[P1582 ] 2. Create one in-app notification and one email per notice type, farm, period and Owner (deduplicated).
[P1583 ] 3. Log the number of notices.
[P1584 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1585]
Scenario | System behavior
Job fails | The next run creates missed notices for the current day only; no duplicates are created.
Owner changed meanwhile | Notices go to the current Owner of the farm.
[/TABLE]
[P1587 3] 24.6 Process Received Bank Evidence (JOB-06)
[P1588 ] Trigger: Queue; runs continuously for evidence stored by API-02 or API-03.
[P1589 ] Purpose: Run the shared payment confirmation for each authenticated evidence exactly once (GBR-72, GBR-73).
[P1590 ] Processing steps:
[P1591 ] 1. Take the next evidence with pending processing.
[P1592 ] 2. Lock processing per farm and evaluate eligibility (direction, receiving account, payment reference, amount, bank-paid time window, allocation, manual income conflict).
[P1593 ] 3. Eligible: commit allocation, PAID, purchased period, service status, receipt and audit in one transaction; create the Owner notification.
[P1594 ] 4. Not eligible: mark the evidence NEEDS_REVIEW with the reason and create a PM reconciliation notification.
[P1595 ] 5. Mark the processing done.
[P1596 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1597]
Scenario | System behavior
Transient error | Retries after 1, 5 and 15 minutes, then hourly for 24 hours; then a PM notification is created. Evidence is never lost.
Repeated processing | Returns the committed result; no second period, receipt or notification (GBR-88).
[/TABLE]
[P1599 3] 24.7 Dispatch Notifications and Emails (JOB-07)
[P1600 ] Trigger: Scheduled every minute.
[P1601 ] Purpose: Deliver notifications and emails for committed business events (GBR-64).
[P1602 ] Processing steps:
[P1603 ] 1. Read undelivered events from the outbox written with each business change.
[P1604 ] 2. Resolve the current recipients and recheck their access.
[P1605 ] 3. Create the in-app notifications and send the emails (API-04); deduplicate by recipient and event.
[P1606 ] 4. Create a reminder notice to the recipient of an internal request that has been OPEN for 4 hours.
[P1607 ] 5. Mark the events delivered.
[P1608 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1609]
Scenario | System behavior
Email provider error | Retries with increasing delay for up to 24 hours; the in-app notification is still created; the business change is never rolled back.
Recipient lost access | The notification is not created; the event is closed.
[/TABLE]
[P1611 3] 24.8 Purge Expired Tokens and Snapshots (JOB-08)
[P1612 ] Trigger: Scheduled daily at 02:00.
[P1613 ] Purpose: Remove technical data that is no longer needed.
[P1614 ] Processing steps:
[P1615 ] 1. Delete verification, reset and invitation link token hashes that expired more than 7 days ago (invitation records and statuses are kept).
[P1616 ] 2. Mark cash report snapshots older than 30 days EXPIRED and delete their rows.
[P1617 ] 3. Process in batches of up to 5,000 records and log the counts.
[P1618 ] Failure behavior:
[TABLE block 1619]
Scenario | System behavior
Timeout during a batch | The batch is rolled back; the job continues at the next run. No business record is affected.
[/TABLE]
[P1621 2] 25. External API Inventory
[P1622 3] 25.1 GET: VietQR image (API-01)
[TABLE block 1623]
Item | Value
URL | GET https://qr.sepay.vn/img?acc={account_number}&bank={bank_code}&amount={amount}&des={payment_reference}
Purpose | Render the VietQR image of a payment request on Transfer Instructions (SCR-59).
Caller | The Owner's browser loads the image URL built by the BeefCare backend from the immutable request snapshot (BeefCare → SePay QR service).
Auth | None (public image service); the URL contains no secret. Displaying the QR confirms nothing.
[/TABLE]
[P1625 3] 25.2 POST: SePay transaction webhook (API-02)
[TABLE block 1626]
Item | Value
URL | POST /api/v1/payments/sepay/webhook (one endpoint per environment: Sandbox, Live)
Purpose | Receive each bank transaction on the receiving account, store it as evidence and start payment confirmation (UC-FE19-05).
Caller | SePay.
Auth | HMAC-SHA256: headers X-SePay-Signature (sha256=hex) and X-SePay-Timestamp; signature over "{timestamp}.{raw body}" with the environment secret; 300-second tolerance.
Data | id (deduplication key), gateway, transactionDate (bank time, Vietnam time), accountNumber, code, content, transferType (in/out), transferAmount, referenceCode, description. Response HTTP 200 with body {"success": true} within 30 seconds after the evidence is stored.
[/TABLE]
[P1628 3] 25.3 GET: SePay transaction lookup (API-03)
[TABLE block 1629]
Item | Value
URL | GET https://my.sepay.vn/userapi/transactions/list (filters: account number, transaction date range, limit) and GET https://my.sepay.vn/userapi/transactions/details/{id}
Purpose | Let the PM find a transaction that was not delivered by webhook and store it as evidence for reconciliation (UC-FE19-03).
Caller | BeefCare backend, on request from the PM workbench (SCR-62).
Auth | Bearer API token per environment, stored server-side only.
[/TABLE]
[P1631 3] 25.4 POST: Transactional email (API-04)
[TABLE block 1632]
Item | Value
URL | Provider send-mail API or SMTP submission (provider to be selected at deployment).
Purpose | Send invitation, verification, password-reset, password-changed, subscription and support emails (JOB-07).
Caller | BeefCare backend.
Auth | Provider API key or SMTP credentials stored server-side; TLS.
[/TABLE]
[P1634 3] 25.5 OAuth 2.0 / OpenID Connect: Google sign-in (API-05)
[TABLE block 1635]
Item | Value
URL | GET https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/v2/auth (scope openid email profile, state, nonce, PKCE); POST https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token (code exchange); callback GET /api/v1/auth/google/callback
Purpose | Authenticate a person with Google for registration, sign-in and invitation acceptance (UC-FE01-01, UC-FE01-02, UC-FE03-03).
Caller | The user's browser (redirects) and the BeefCare backend (code exchange).
Auth | OAuth client ID and secret stored server-side; the ID token signature and claims (iss, aud, exp, nonce, email_verified) are validated.
[/TABLE]
[P1637 1] IV. Non-Functional Requirements
[P1638 2] 1. External Interfaces
[TABLE block 1639]
Interface | Description
User interface | One responsive web application in Vietnamese for desktop, tablet and phone browsers. No native application is needed.
Phone camera | QR ear tags are scanned with the phone camera in the browser. A code can also be entered by hand.
Printer | QR ear tags are printed on a standard printer.
Google sign-in | Users can sign in with a Google account through OAuth 2.0 and OpenID Connect.
SePay | SePay sends the bank transactions of the subscription account and provides the VietQR images for payment.
Email | The system sends verification, password reset, invitation and notification emails.
Network | All traffic between browsers, the system and external services uses HTTPS.
[/TABLE]
[P1641 2] 2. Quality Attributes
[P1642 3] 2.1 Usability
[TABLE block 1643]
Requirement | Description
Quick identification | A Farm Technician opens the profile of a cattle by scanning its tag from the home screen, without searching lists.
Short recording | A routine event is recorded from the cattle profile in one dialog that shows only the needed fields.
Phone use | On phones, tables become cards and dialogs open full screen.
Clear labels | Measured and estimated values, unknown history and tentative and confirmed dates are labelled differently.
Clear errors | Error messages say what to correct. A failed save is never shown as successful.
Role-based actions | Actions the role cannot perform are hidden. The current farm and role are always shown.
[/TABLE]
[P1645 3] 2.2 Performance
[TABLE block 1646]
Requirement | Target | Condition
Page load time | Any page loads within 3 seconds | Up to 100 users active at the same time
Form submission | The system responds within 2 seconds | Up to 100 users active at the same time
QR scan | The cattle profile opens within 2 seconds after the code is read | Up to 100 users active at the same time
Dashboard and reports | Results are shown within 5 seconds | A farm of about 300 cattle with 2 years of data
Maximum simultaneous users | The system stays fully functional for 100 users | Single server deployment
[/TABLE]
[P1648 3] 2.3 Security
[TABLE block 1649]
Requirement | Description
Authentication required | Every page and action requires sign-in, except the landing page, sign-in, registration, email verification, password reset and the SePay webhook.
Authorization | The server checks the account status, farm membership, role and farm service status on every request. Hiding a button is never the only protection.
Farm data isolation | A user never sees data of a farm the user cannot access.
Password protection | Passwords are stored only as Argon2 hashes and are never logged or returned.
Account lockout | After 5 consecutive failed attempts, password sign-in is locked for 15 minutes.
Email links | Verification links are valid for 24 hours and reset links for 30 minutes. Each link can be used once.
Sessions | A session ends on sign-out, password change, account suspension, or after 8 hours of inactivity, or 30 days with Remember me.
Payment webhook | SePay transactions are accepted only with a valid HMAC-SHA256 signature sent within 300 seconds.
Platform staff access | Customer Support, Platform Manager and System Admin see only the data of their functions and never cattle or care records.
Audit | Important changes are recorded with the user and the time and cannot be modified.
[/TABLE]
[P1651 1] V. Requirement Appendix
[P1652 2] 1. Business Rules
[P1653 3] 1.1 Access, Accounts and Roles
[TABLE block 1654]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-01 | One person has one BeefCare account, identified by a verified email. Google sign-in and a password with the same email belong to the same account. The phone number is contact data only. | FE-01, FE-03 | Avoids duplicate accounts. | System, on save
GBR-02 | A password has 8 to 128 characters and differs from the email. It is stored only as a one-way hash. After 5 failed attempts, password sign-in is locked for 15 minutes; Google sign-in stays available. | FE-01 | Protects accounts against guessing. | System, on sign-in
GBR-03 | A verification link is valid for 24 hours and a reset link for 30 minutes. Each link is single-use and a new link replaces the previous one. A reset request gives the same answer whether or not the email exists. | FE-01 | Limits misuse of links and hides which emails exist. | System, on link use
GBR-04 | Only an ACTIVE account can sign in. Suspending an account ends its sessions at the next request. Restoring an account does not restore revoked roles or memberships. | FE-01, FE-03 | Suspension takes effect at once. | System, on every request
GBR-05 | A Guest becomes a Farm Owner by completing Owner registration. Owner registration grants no farm or service; a farm is created only by Register Farm. | FE-01, FE-02 | Separates the person from the farm. | System, on save
GBR-06 | Each farm role has a fixed set of permissions. Farm Owner: all farm data and actions. Farm Manager: records and corrects care, reproduction and feeding, maintains pens, herd entry, cattle profiles, catalogues and parameters and writes announcements to Technicians. Farm Technician: read-only access. | FE-03 to FE-18, FE-23 | Simple roles for small farms. | System, on every request
GBR-07 | Only the Farm Owner manages members, the subscription and payments, herd exit vouchers and their collections, the production purpose of cattle and the farm information. | FE-02, FE-03, FE-05, FE-13, FE-19 | Ownership and money decisions stay with the owner. | System, on every request
GBR-08 | Care and reproduction events are recorded, corrected and deleted by the Farm Owner or a Farm Manager. A Farm Technician may be named as observer or performer. | FE-08 to FE-12 | One accountable recorder per event. | System, on save
GBR-09 | Every request rechecks the account status, the membership and role in the farm, the platform role and the farm service status. A link, notification or QR code is never proof of permission. | All features | Access changes apply at once. | System, on every request
GBR-10 | Only a System Admin grants, changes, revokes or restores platform access (CS, PM, SA). A user has at most one platform role and at least one ACTIVE System Admin always remains. | FE-03 | Keeps platform administration under control. | System, on save
GBR-11 | Platform staff never get farm operational access and never see cattle, care or reproduction records. | FE-02, FE-17, FE-19 to FE-21 | Protects farm data. | System, on every request
[/TABLE]
[P1656 3] 1.2 Farm, Service and Membership
[TABLE block 1657]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-12 | Each farm is an independent tenant with one Owner. An Owner may own several farms. Data of one farm is never shown in another farm. | All farm features | Keeps farms separate. | System, on every request
GBR-13 | Farm service status is PENDING_PAYMENT until the first accepted payment, ACTIVE while a purchased period covers today, EXPIRED after coverage ends and SUSPENDED while a suspension is in force. | FE-02, FE-19 | One clear rule for service availability. | System, on every request
GBR-14 | Farm operations need an ACTIVE service. While the farm is not ACTIVE, the Owner can still view and update farm information, pay, use support and withdraw member access. Managers and Technicians cannot open the farm. | FE-02 to FE-21 | The Owner can always pay and get help. | System, on every request
GBR-15 | Payment and suspension are independent. A payment never lifts a suspension and a restore never extends coverage. Suspend and restore need a reason. | FE-02, FE-19 | Commercial and administrative decisions do not override each other. | System, on save
GBR-16 | Only the Owner invites, for an ACTIVE farm, a Farm Manager or Farm Technician. One pending invitation per email. An invitation is valid for 3, 7 or 14 days from the last send; resending restarts the validity. | FE-03 | Controlled onboarding of staff. | System, on save
GBR-17 | An invitation is accepted only by the account with the invited email while it is valid. Acceptance creates one membership; a user has at most one membership per farm. | FE-03 | A forwarded link cannot give access to someone else. | System, on acceptance
GBR-18 | The Owner membership cannot change. Other members can switch between Manager and Technician or be deactivated and reactivated, with a date and reason. A membership is never deleted. | FE-03 | Keeps the history of who did what. | System, on save
[/TABLE]
[P1659 3] 1.3 Pens, Cattle Identity and Herd Lifecycle
[TABLE block 1660]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-19 | A pen code is unique in the farm and the capacity is at least 1 and not below the headcount. A used pen is made INACTIVE, never deleted. | FE-04 | Matches the real barn. | System, on save
GBR-20 | Cattle cannot be placed or moved into an INACTIVE or full pen. Stopping a pen does not move its cattle. | FE-04, FE-06 | Capacity is a physical limit. | System, on save
GBR-21 | Pen reception criteria are advisory. When a cattle does not meet them, the user must enter a reason to continue. A change of criteria creates a new version for future placements. | FE-04, FE-06 | Allows exceptions while keeping them traceable. | System, on save
GBR-22 | A cattle is in one pen at a time. A move closes the current assignment and opens the new one; the move date is not before the current assignment and not in the future. | FE-04 | Accurate pen history. | System, on save
GBR-23 | The internal cattle ID and the display code are generated once and never reused. The ear tag number is editable and unique among ACTIVE cattle of the farm. | FE-05, FE-06 | One permanent identity per animal. | System, on save
GBR-24 | Herd status, production purpose, life stage, pen, health state and reproductive status are separate. Pen, health, meat withdrawal, reproductive status and herd group are derived from records and never edited on the profile. The herd group is the first match of: Calves, Fattening, Breeding bulls, Breeding females, Young stock, Other. | FE-05, FE-09, FE-15 | Each value has one source and each animal is counted once. | Design rule
GBR-25 | Only the Owner changes the production purpose. The Owner or a Manager changes the life stage and identification. A SIRE purpose is only for males and a BREEDING purpose only for females. Each change needs a date and reason. Life stage changes are suggested, never applied automatically. | FE-05, FE-06, FE-14 | Every change is explained in the history. | System, on save
GBR-26 | EXITED and VOIDED cattle are read-only. A cattle on a pending death voucher accepts no new event until the Owner confirms or dismisses the voucher. | FE-04 to FE-16 | No data is added to an animal that has left. | System, on save
GBR-27 | A cattle has one ACTIVE QR tag. A replacement needs a reason and the old tag becomes REPLACED. Scanning a replaced tag still opens the same cattle. | FE-05 | A lost tag does not break traceability. | System, on save
GBR-28 | A QR code is not permission. A scan finds a cattle only in a farm the user can access; there is no public cattle page. | FE-05 | Keeps farm data private. | System, on scan
GBR-29 | A cattle enters the herd once, through a purchase voucher or a farm-born calves voucher. A repeated submission returns the first result. A returning animal gets a new ID. | FE-06 | No duplicate animals. | System, on save
GBR-30 | Vaccination, disease and reproduction history before entry is declared as Known, None or Unknown; Unknown is never shown as negative. A declared pregnancy is shown as not checked until a pregnancy check is recorded. | FE-06 | Honest records of bought-in cattle. | System, on save
GBR-31 | A farm-born calf takes its birth date, dam, sire and proposed pen from the calving. Only a live calf can be registered. | FE-06, FE-12 | Links the calf to its parents without re-entry. | System, on save
GBR-32 | Posting an entry voucher creates every cattle with its entry, history, first pen and QR tag together; an error on one line posts nothing. New cattle join a vaccination schedule only when a user adds them. | FE-06, FE-09 | No partial animals. | System, on save
GBR-33 | The entry of one cattle is corrected with a reason, keeping the old values. An entry or voucher can be voided only when the cattle have no other events. A voided cattle leaves all lists and counts. | FE-06 | Entry mistakes can be fixed safely. | System, on save
GBR-34 | Only the Owner creates, confirms, dismisses or voids exit vouchers. The exit date is not in the future and not before the latest event of the cattle. A case closed as Died or Missing creates a pending death voucher; the cattle stays ACTIVE until the Owner decides. | FE-13, FE-10 | Removing an animal is an ownership decision. | System, on save
GBR-35 | Confirming an exit sets the cattle EXITED, closes the pen, cancels future doses and reminders, closes open health cases and an open pregnancy. The Owner sees these effects as warnings before confirming. | FE-13 | A complete end of the animal record. | System, on confirmation
GBR-36 | Voiding a confirmed exit needs a reason and returns each cattle to ACTIVE in its last pen, or another pen when that one is full or inactive. Closed items are listed but not restored. | FE-13 | Mistakes are fixed without losing history. | System, on save
GBR-37 | The cattle timeline is a read-only view of the source records in date order. Corrected and deleted records are shown only with Show corrections. | FE-16 | One trustworthy history per animal. | Design rule
[/TABLE]
[P1662 3] 1.4 Growth, Vaccination and Health
[TABLE block 1663]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-38 | A growth record has a weight, a BCS or both. A weight is in kg with a method and a basis Measured or Estimated. The time is not in the future or before herd entry. | FE-08 | Comparable and honest growth data. | System, on save
GBR-39 | Growth indicators use valid records only and always show whether a weight is estimated. Tape weight = 88.4 × girth (m)² × length (m). ADG is shown only when two weights are at least 14 days apart. | FE-08, FE-15 | Transparent calculations. | System, on calculation
GBR-40 | A wrong growth record is edited or deleted; the original values are kept and deleted records are not used. | FE-08 | Mistakes are fixed without rewriting history. | System, on save
GBR-41 | A vaccination schedule targets selected ACTIVE cattle and creates one planned dose per cattle and dose number. Changes affect only doses not yet given; stopping cancels the planned doses. | FE-09 | Planning is explicit and history stays true. | System, on save
GBR-42 | A vaccination record is saved per cattle. An on-farm record may complete a planned dose; a historical record never does. | FE-09 | Each dose is traceable to one animal. | System, on save
GBR-43 | A planned dose is upcoming, due within the reminder lead time, or overdue after its date. Doses are not deferred; a cattle that cannot be vaccinated is removed from the schedule. | FE-09 | Schedules show what is really planned. | System, on display
GBR-44 | A wrong vaccination record is edited or deleted with the original values kept. Deleting an on-farm record returns its dose to planned. | FE-09 | Keeps the schedule consistent. | System, on save
GBR-45 | A health case is OPEN, CHRONIC or CLOSED with outcome Recovered, Died, Missing or Closed by exit. A closed case can be reopened with a reason. Health state: Under treatment when an open case has a treatment, Monitoring when a case is open or chronic, otherwise Healthy. | FE-10 | Health state always comes from cases. | System, on save
GBR-46 | A treatment records the medicine actually given. Meat withdrawal end = last administration + withdrawal days. The cattle shows the latest end among its treatments and on-farm vaccinations. BeefCare does not diagnose or prescribe. | FE-09, FE-10, FE-13 | Food-safety traceability. | System, on save
GBR-47 | Closing a case as Died or Missing creates a pending death voucher; the cattle is not exited automatically. A vaccination reaction is a health case linked to the vaccination. | FE-09, FE-10, FE-13 | Death is confirmed by the Owner. | System, on save
[/TABLE]
[P1665 3] 1.5 Reproduction
[TABLE block 1666]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-48 | Reproduction events belong to one female of the farm. An internal sire is a male of the same farm. | FE-11, FE-12 | Prevents impossible links. | System, on save
GBR-49 | Estrus, breeding, pregnancy check, update and calving are separate real events. A reminder or elapsed time never proves an event. Event times cannot be in the future. | FE-11, FE-12, FE-14 | Factual records. | System, on save
GBR-50 | Linking a breeding to an estrus and a check to a breeding is optional; the system proposes the latest one. | FE-11, FE-12 | Farms often record late. | System, on save
GBR-51 | Only a Pregnant check opens a pregnancy. Not pregnant returns the female to awaiting breeding. Inconclusive plans a re-check. | FE-12 | Pregnancy must be confirmed by a check. | System, on save
GBR-52 | A female has at most one open pregnancy. A closed pregnancy is never reopened by a normal update. | FE-12 | No contradictory pregnancies. | System, on save
GBR-53 | Expected calving date = breeding time + gestation length (default 283 days), tentative until a Pregnant check confirms it. Without a breeding, it is estimated from the months pregnant at the check. | FE-11, FE-12, FE-14 | Planning with a clear basis. | System, on save
GBR-54 | Reproductive status applies to ACTIVE young stock and adult females of purpose Breeding or Undecided. It is derived from the latest events: Awaiting breeding, In estrus, Bred, Pregnant or Post-calving. The actions offered follow the status. | FE-11, FE-12 | Users see only actions that make sense. | System, on display
GBR-55 | Breeding eligibility is advisory: Met when the minimum age and weight, the rest after calving and no open health case are met. A real breeding can still be recorded. | FE-11, FE-18 | Decision support, not a block. | System, on display
GBR-56 | A pregnancy update is an observation, complication or loss. A loss closes the pregnancy and returns the female to awaiting breeding. | FE-12 | Separates reproduction from health care. | System, on save
GBR-57 | A calving saves the dam, birth time, course and one outcome per calf together and closes the pregnancy. It can be recorded without a tracked pregnancy. | FE-12 | Complete birth records. | System, on save
GBR-58 | Each live calf is saved as a draft or registered at once. A draft calf waits for confirmation, has a unique tag and receives no events until registered. | FE-12, FE-06 | Calves are registered without duplicates. | System, on save
GBR-59 | A reproduction record is edited or deleted only when no later record depends on it. History is never deleted in cascade. | FE-11, FE-12 | Fixes errors while keeping data consistent. | System, on save
[/TABLE]
[P1668 3] 1.6 Coordination, Reminders and Reporting
[TABLE block 1669]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-60 | The Owner writes to Managers and Technicians; a Manager writes only to Technicians. Platform staff are never recipients. | FE-07 | Announcements go from higher to lower roles. | System, on publish
GBR-61 | Drafts are visible only to their author. Only the author corrects an own announcement. | FE-07 | Protects authorship. | System, on every request
GBR-62 | Publishing saves a fixed version and delivers it once to each recipient. A correction needs a reason and creates a new version for the same recipients. | FE-07 | Recipients see exactly what was published. | System, on publish
GBR-63 | Reminders come only from the rules in Table V-1. Each has one source record. Reading a reminder never resolves it; correcting or voiding the source updates it. | FE-14 | Reminders are traceable to real data. | System, scheduled job
GBR-64 | A notification has one recipient and one target. Opening it rechecks access. Sending never repeats or undoes the business change. | FE-14 | Correct delivery without leaks. | System, on read
GBR-65 | The dashboard and reports are for the Owner and Managers only. Each cattle is counted once per indicator and draft calves are excluded. | FE-15 | Correct management figures. | System, on calculation
GBR-66 | Dates, periods and scheduled jobs use UTC+07:00. A date range covers whole days. | All features | Correct period boundaries. | System, on calculation
GBR-67 | Catalogue codes are unique in the farm. A used item is made inactive, never deleted; an unused vaccine may be deleted. Editing an item never changes past records. The purpose type is fixed once used. | FE-18 | Stable master data. | System, on save
GBR-68 | Parameters are saved as a new version with a source and an effective date. A new version never changes recorded events. | FE-18 | Parameters change without rewriting history. | System, on save

[P1671 3] 1.7 Subscription and Payment
[TABLE block 1672]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-69 | One plan per farm, sold for 1, 2 or 3 years at 1,200,000, 2,160,000 and 3,060,000 VND. Payment is a bank transfer by the Owner; there is no automatic renewal. | FE-19, FE-22 | Simple pricing for small farms. | System, on quote
GBR-70 | A payment request is fixed once created and valid for 24 hours. A farm has at most one pending request. It can be cancelled while no transfer has been received. | FE-19 | One clear transfer instruction at a time. | System, on save
GBR-71 | A renewal can be requested only in the last 90 days of coverage or after it ends. No request while the farm is SUSPENDED. | FE-19 | Prevents paying years ahead by mistake. | System, on quote
GBR-72 | A payment becomes PAID only with an authenticated incoming transfer of the exact amount, to the receiving account, inside the request window and matching one request by its payment code, or selected by the Platform Manager with a reason. One transfer pays one request. | FE-19 | Money is accepted once and only when it matches. | System, on confirmation
GBR-73 | A purchased period starts at the later of the confirmation time and the current end, and lasts the paid number of years. Periods never change. | FE-19 | Early renewal never loses days. | System, on confirmation
GBR-74 | Every authenticated bank transaction is kept once as evidence. Unaccepted evidence never grants service. The Platform Manager accepts, holds or rejects it with a reason. Refunds are made outside BeefCare. | FE-19 | Full traceability of money received. | System, on save
GBR-75 | The Platform Manager publishes prices as a full version, now or from a future time. Issued requests and periods keep their price. | FE-19, FE-22 | Controlled price changes. | System, on save
[/TABLE]
