GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-76 | Only a Farm Owner creates tickets, about the own account or an owned farm. Customer Support sets the priority. Support stays available when the farm is not ACTIVE. | FE-20 | Owners can always get help. | System, on save
GBR-77 | Only the assigned Customer Support changes a ticket. Claiming and handing over are recorded; a handover needs a reason. | FE-20 | One accountable person per ticket. | System, on save
GBR-78 | Ticket status: OPEN, IN_PROGRESS, WAITING_CUSTOMER, RESOLVED, CLOSED. An Owner reply returns a waiting ticket to IN_PROGRESS; a reopen returns a resolved ticket to OPEN. A closed ticket is read-only. | FE-20 | Clear ticket lifecycle. | System, on save
GBR-79 | Customer Support may send one open internal request per role to a Platform Manager or System Admin. The recipient sees only the request, never the conversation. An open request blocks resolving and closing. | FE-20 | Specialists see only what they need. | System, on save
GBR-80 | Each resolution is a new version. A ticket is closed only after the Owner confirms the latest resolution and no internal request is open. Tickets are never closed by timeout. | FE-20 | Closing reflects the customer agreement. | System, on save
GBR-81 | The Owner sees only own tickets and public messages. Internal notes are for Customer Support only. Internal requests are visible only to the assigned Customer Support and the recipient. | FE-20, FE-14 | Private internal discussion. | System, on every request
[/TABLE]
[P1677 3] 1.9 Platform Finance, Audit and Common Rules
[TABLE block 1678]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-82 | A subscription receipt is a read-only view of a paid request. It cannot be edited, voided or entered again as manual income. | FE-21 | One source of subscription income. | Design rule
GBR-83 | Manual finance records are created by the Platform Manager. A draft can be edited or discarded; a posted record is fixed and can only be voided with a reason. | FE-21 | Auditable bookkeeping. | System, on save
GBR-84 | A bank transaction cannot be both a subscription receipt and manual income. | FE-19, FE-21 | No double counting. | System, on save
GBR-85 | Net cash flow = subscription receipts + posted income − posted expense in the period. Drafts, voided records and unpaid requests are excluded. It is cash flow, not profit. | FE-21 | Clear platform cash view. | System, on calculation
GBR-86 | Important changes are recorded in the audit log with the user, role and time. Only a System Admin views it, and never clinical data, ticket bodies or secrets. | All features | Accountability without exposing sensitive data. | System, on save
GBR-87 | The public page shows only published content, the prices in effect and the official contact. When prices cannot be loaded, it shows unavailable, never an old price. | FE-22 | Accurate public information. | System, on display
GBR-88 | A repeated request returns the first result. A record, its history and its events are saved together or not at all. | All features | No duplicates on poor connections. | System, on save
GBR-89 | An update is rejected when the record changed since the user opened it; the user reloads and reviews. | All features | No silent overwrites between users. | System, on save
[/TABLE]
[P1680 3] 1.10 Herd Entry and Exit Vouchers
[TABLE block 1681]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-90 | An entry voucher is Purchase or Farm-born calves with one line per cattle. A draft has no effect. Posting checks every line and creates the cattle; lines can then no longer be added or removed. | FE-06 | No half-entered animals. | System, on post
GBR-91 | Line amount = price × weight or price per head. Outstanding = total − recorded payments. A payment is a positive amount, not above the outstanding amount, and is voided, never edited. These amounts are farm bookkeeping, not invoices. | FE-06, FE-13, FE-23 | The farm knows what it owes and is owed. | System, on save
GBR-92 | The pricing basis applies to all lines; per kg needs a weight on every line. Each weight is Measured or Estimated. Voucher weights are not growth records. | FE-06, FE-13 | Prices match how cattle are traded. | System, on save
GBR-93 | A farm-born calves voucher contains only pending live calves. Each calf enters on its birth date as an unweaned calf with the Undecided purpose by default. The voucher has no amounts. | FE-06, FE-12 | Calves enter with the same document as bought cattle. | System, on save
GBR-94 | Only ACTIVE cattle on no other exit voucher can be added. Warnings never block; the Owner must tick the confirmation. If any cattle is no longer ACTIVE, nothing is confirmed. | FE-13 | Exit decisions are taken with full information. | System, on confirmation
GBR-95 | A pending death voucher is confirmed by the Owner with the cause and carcass disposal, or dismissed with a reason, which reopens the case. It has no amounts. The Owner may also record a death directly. | FE-13, FE-10 | Deaths are confirmed with evidence. | System, on confirmation
GBR-96 | Cattle lines can be imported from the Excel template, max 200 rows. Each row is checked like a manual line; rows with errors are not added. | FE-06 | Fast entry without skipping checks. | System, on import
GBR-97 | Sellers, buyers and suppliers belong to the farm with a unique name. A used partner is made inactive, never deleted. | FE-06, FE-13, FE-23 | One contact list per farm. | System, on save
[/TABLE]
[P1683 3] 1.11 Feed Management
[TABLE block 1684]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-98 | A feed item code is unique and its stock unit is kg. Code and unit cannot change after the first movement. A used item is made inactive, never deleted. | FE-23 | Stable units keep stock correct. | System, on save
GBR-99 | Stock changes only through receipts, adjustments and feeding records and never becomes negative. Posting a receipt updates the weighted average cost. A receipt can be voided only while the stock still covers it. | FE-23 | Stock is explained by documents. | System, on post
GBR-100 | A stock adjustment records the difference between counted and system stock with a reason. Posted adjustments are fixed. | FE-23 | Counts are reconciled without rewriting history. | System, on post
GBR-101 | A ration sets the kg per head of each feed for each session. A change is a new version from tomorrow; one version is effective per pen and date. Recorded feeding never changes. | FE-23 | The plan changes while the record stays true. | System, on save
GBR-102 | A feeding record is kept per pen, date and session. Actual kg is not above the stock and is deducted from it. Corrections need a reason and adjust the stock. | FE-23 | Daily feeding is recorded once. | System, on save
GBR-103 | The Owner and Managers manage all feed functions. Technicians view rations and the feeding log. | FE-23 | Feeding follows care responsibilities. | System, on every request
GBR-104 | A low-stock reminder is raised when stock is at or below the minimum. A feeding reminder is raised 2 hours after a session time with no record. | FE-23, FE-14 | Shortages and missed feedings are seen in time. | System, scheduled job
GBR-105 | Feed cost = actual kg × average cost. Days of cover = stock ÷ average daily use of 7 days. Losses from adjustments are reported separately. | FE-23 | Reproducible feed figures. | System, on calculation
[/TABLE]
[P1686 3] 1.12 Herd Performance
[TABLE block 1687]
GBR-ID | Rule Statement | Applies To | Rationale | Enforced By
GBR-106 | Reproduction indicators use valid events and show the number of cases; fewer than 3 shows Insufficient data. They include calving interval, days open, services per conception, conception rates, pregnancy loss rate, age at first calving and females overdue for conception. | FE-15, FE-11 | Breeding herds are judged by these figures. | System, on calculation
GBR-107 | Fattening performance covers fattening cattle in the period: gain, ADG, feed and feed cost per kg of gain. Pen feed is shared equally among the cattle of the pen. A projected finishing date is shown only with a target weight and a positive ADG. No profit is calculated. | FE-15, FE-08 | Feeding decisions need gain and feed cost. | System, on calculation
GBR-109 | A new farm gets the default parameters and a starter catalogue of production purposes, common breeds, vaccines and feed items. Reference medicines are not pre-created. Starter items belong to the farm. | FE-02, FE-18, FE-23 | A new farm can start recording at once. | System, on farm registration
[/TABLE]
[P1689 3] 1.13 Reminder Rules
[P1690 ] Table V-1 Reminder rules
[TABLE block 1691]
Rule | Name | Created when | Due | Closed when | For
REM-01 | Weighing due | ACTIVE cattle. | Latest weight date + weighing interval, 30 days. | A new weight is recorded, or the cattle exits. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-02 | Breeding window | Estrus of a female without an open pregnancy. | 8 to 12 hours after the estrus. | A breeding is recorded, a newer estrus is recorded, or the window passes. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-03 | Pregnancy check due | Breeding without a later check, or a pregnancy declared at entry. | Breeding + 28 days, or entry + 7 days. | A check is recorded or the breeding is deleted. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-04 | Pregnancy re-check | Inconclusive check. | Check + 14 days. | The next check is recorded. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-05 | Upcoming calving | Expected calving date. | From 30 days before the date. | A calving, a loss or a Not pregnant check is recorded, or the cattle exits. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-06 | Vaccination dose | Planned dose. | Due 7 days before the planned date, overdue after it. | The dose is given, the schedule stops or the cattle exits. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-07 | Ready for breeding after calving | Calving. | Calving + 45 days. | An estrus or breeding is recorded, or the cattle exits. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-08 | Pen move or life stage suggestion | Cattle no longer meets the criteria of its pen, or reaches the age of the next life stage or a first calving. | At once, or on the date the age is reached. | The cattle is moved or its stage is changed, or it exits. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-09 | Draft calf awaiting confirmation | Calf saved as draft. | At once. | The calf is registered or marked died before registration. | Owner, Manager
REM-10 | Low feed stock | Stock at or below the minimum level. | At once. | Stock rises above the minimum or the item becomes inactive. | Owner, Manager
REM-11 | Feeding session not recorded | Session of today's ration of a pen with cattle. | Session time + 2 hours. | The session is recorded, or the day ends. | Owner, Manager, Technician
REM-12 | Death or loss awaiting confirmation | Pending death voucher. | At once. | The Owner confirms or dismisses the voucher. | Owner, Manager
REM-13 | Finishing weight reached | Fattening cattle at or above the target weight. | Date of the weight. | The cattle exits or its purpose changes. | Owner, Manager
[/TABLE]

[TABLE block 1697]
ID | Other Requirement
OR-01 | Decision support only: BeefCare shows reminders, warnings and summaries but never decides breeding, sale, culling or treatment and never records an event by itself. Veterinarians, AI technicians and buyers have no accounts; their names and results are recorded by the Owner or a Manager.
OR-02 | QR ear tag: the printed tag shows the display code above the QR code, and the QR stays valid when the ear tag number changes. Cattle QR tags and payment VietQR codes look different.
OR-03 | Data retention: cattle, care, voucher, membership, payment, ticket and finance records stay available to authorized users after exit, deactivation or service expiry. No automatic deletion is defined.
OR-04 | Scope: an Owner may own several independent farms. In scope are herd, care, reproduction, feed management, farm voucher bookkeeping, subscription, support and basic platform cash records. Out of scope are other inventory, invoices and tax documents, farm accounting, payroll, IoT devices and native mobile apps.
OR-05 | SePay environments: Sandbox uses simulated transactions and Live uses real transfers, with separate accounts and secrets and the same business rules.
[/TABLE]
